<h1 id="genesis"> Genesis </h1>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_1"> 1 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_1_front">
<h3> The Creation
 </h3>
<span> (John 1:1–5; Hebrews 11:1–3)
 </span>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>In the beginning God created the heavens and the earth.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Now the earth was formless and void, and darkness was over the surface of the deep. And the Spirit of God was hovering over the surface of the waters.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>And God said, “Let there be light,” <span> <a href="#genesis_1_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
and there was light.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>And God saw that the light was good, and He separated the light from the darkness.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>God called the light “day,” and the darkness He called “night.”
And there was evening, and there was morning—the first day.<span> <a href="#genesis_1_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>And God said, “Let there be an expanse <span> <a href="#genesis_1_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
between the waters, to separate the waters from the waters.”
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>So God made the expanse and separated the waters beneath it from the waters above. And it was so.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>God called the expanse “sky.”
And there was evening, and there was morning—the second day.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>And God said, “Let the waters under the sky be gathered into one place, so that the dry land may appear.” And it was so.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>God called the dry land “earth,” and the gathering of waters He called “seas.” And God saw that it was good.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Then God said, “Let the earth bring forth vegetation: seed-bearing plants and fruit trees, each bearing fruit with seed according to its kind.” And it was so.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>The earth produced vegetation: seed-bearing plants according to their kinds and trees bearing fruit with seed according to their kinds. And God saw that it was good.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>And there was evening, and there was morning—the third day.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>And God said, “Let there be lights in the expanse of the sky to distinguish between the day and the night, and let them be signs to mark the seasons and days and years.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And let them serve as lights in the expanse of the sky to shine upon the earth.” And it was so.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>God made two great lights: the greater light to rule the day and the lesser light to rule the night. And He made the stars as well.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>God set these lights in the expanse of the sky to shine upon the earth,
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>to preside over the day and the night, and to separate the light from the darkness. And God saw that it was good.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>And there was evening, and there was morning—the fourth day.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>And God said, “Let the waters teem with living creatures, and let birds fly above the earth in the open expanse of the sky.”
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>So God created the great sea creatures and every living thing that moves, with which the waters teemed according to their kinds, and every winged bird after its kind. And God saw that it was good.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Then God blessed them and said, “Be fruitful and multiply and fill the waters of the seas, and let birds multiply on the earth.”
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>And there was evening, and there was morning—the fifth day.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>And God said, “Let the earth bring forth living creatures according to their kinds: livestock, land crawlers, and beasts of the earth according to their kinds.” And it was so.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>God made the beasts of the earth according to their kinds, the livestock according to their kinds, and everything that crawls upon the earth according to its kind. And God saw that it was good.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Then God said, “Let Us make man in Our image, after Our likeness, to rule over the fish of the sea and the birds of the air, over the livestock, and over all the earth itself <span> <a href="#genesis_1_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
and every creature that crawls upon it.”
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>So God created man in His own image;
in the image of God He created him;
male and female He created them.<span> <a href="#genesis_1_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>God blessed them and said to them, “Be fruitful and multiply, and fill the earth and subdue it; rule over the fish of the sea and the birds of the air and every creature that crawls upon the earth.”
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Then God said, “Behold, I have given you every seed-bearing plant on the face of all the earth, and every tree whose fruit contains seed. They will be yours for food.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>And to every beast of the earth and every bird of the air and every creature that crawls upon the earth—everything that has the breath of life in it—I have given every green plant for food.” And it was so.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_1_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>And God looked upon all that He had made, and indeed, it was very good.
And there was evening, and there was morning—the sixth day.
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_1_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_1_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>1:3</em>: Cited in 2 Corinthians 4:6 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_1_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>1:5</em>: Literally day one </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_1_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>1:6</em>: Or a canopy or a firmament or a vault; also in verses 7, 8, 14, 15, 17, and 20 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_1_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>1:26</em>: MT; Syriac and over all the beasts of the earth </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_1_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>1:27</em>: Cited in Matthew 19:4 and Mark 10:6 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_2"> 2 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_2_front">
<h3> The Seventh Day
 </h3>
<span> (Exodus 16:22–30; Hebrews 4:1–11)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Thus the heavens and the earth were completed in all their vast array. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And by the seventh day God had finished the work He had been doing; so on that day He rested from all His work.<span> <a href="#genesis_2_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Then God blessed the seventh day and sanctified it, because on that day He rested from all the work of creation that He had accomplished. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_2_3_section">
<h3> Man and Woman in the Garden
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>This is the account of the heavens and the earth when they were created, in the day that the LORD <span> <a href="#genesis_2_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
God made them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Now no shrub of the field had yet appeared on the earth, nor had any plant of the field sprouted, for the LORD God had not yet sent rain upon the earth, and there was no man to cultivate the ground. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>But springs <span> <a href="#genesis_2_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
welled up from the earth and watered the whole surface of the ground. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD God formed man from the dust of the ground and breathed the breath of life into his nostrils, and the man became a living being.<span> <a href="#genesis_2_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>And the LORD God planted a garden in Eden, in the east, where He placed the man He had formed. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Out of the ground the LORD God gave growth to every tree that is pleasing to the eye and good for food. And in the middle of the garden were the tree of life and the tree of the knowledge of good and evil. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Now a river flowed out of Eden to water the garden, and from there it branched into four headwaters: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>The name of the first river is the Pishon; it winds through the whole land of Havilah, where there is gold. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>And the gold of that land is pure, and bdellium and onyx are found there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>The name of the second river is the Gihon; it winds through the whole land of Cush. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>The name of the third river is the Tigris; it runs along the east side of Assyria. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD God took the man and placed him in the Garden of Eden to cultivate and keep it. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>And the LORD God commanded him, “You may eat freely from every tree of the garden, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>but you must not eat from the tree of the knowledge of good and evil; for in the day that you eat of it, you will surely die.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>The LORD God also said, “It is not good for the man to be alone. I will make for him a suitable helper.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>And out of the ground the LORD God formed every beast of the field and every bird of the air, and He brought them to the man to see what he would name each one. And whatever the man called each living creature, that was its name. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>The man gave names to all the livestock, to the birds of the air, and to every beast of the field. But for Adam <span> <a href="#genesis_2_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
no suitable helper was found. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>So the LORD God caused the man to fall into a deep sleep, and while he slept, He took one of the man’s ribs <span> <a href="#genesis_2_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
and closed up the area with flesh. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And from the rib that the LORD God had taken from the man, He made a woman and brought her to him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>And the man said: 
“This is now bone of my bones 
and flesh of my flesh; 
she shall be called ‘woman,’ 
for out of man she was taken.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>For this reason a man will leave his father and mother and be united to his wife, and they will become one flesh.<span> <a href="#genesis_2_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_2_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>And the man and his wife were both naked, and they were not ashamed. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_2_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_2_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>2:2</em>: Cited in Hebrews 4:4 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_2_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>2:4</em>: LORD or GOD, with capital letters, represents the proper name of the God of Israel and the one true God, transliterated from the Hebrew as YHWH; here and throughout the Scriptures. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_2_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>2:6</em>: Or mist </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_2_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>2:7</em>: Or a living soul; cited in 1 Corinthians 15:45 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_2_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>2:20</em>: Or the man, as in verses 19 and 21 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_2_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>2:21</em>: Or took part of the man’s side; similarly in verse 22 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_2_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>2:24</em>: LXX and the two will become one flesh; cited in Matthew 19:5, Mark 10:7–8, 1 Corinthians 6:16, and Ephesians 5:31 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_3"> 3 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_3_front">
<h3> The Serpent’s Deception
 </h3>
<span> (Romans 5:12–21)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now the serpent <span> <a href="#genesis_3_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
was more crafty than any beast of the field that the LORD God had made. And he said to the woman, “Did God really say, ‘You must not eat from any tree in the garden?’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>The woman answered the serpent, “We may eat the fruit of the trees of the garden, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>but about the fruit of the tree in the middle of the garden, God has said, ‘You must not eat of it or touch it, or you will die.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>“You will not surely die,” the serpent told the woman. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>“For God knows that in the day you eat of it, your eyes will be opened and you will be like God, knowing good and evil.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>When the woman saw that the tree was good for food and pleasing to the eyes, and that it was desirable for obtaining wisdom, she took the fruit and ate it. She also gave some to her husband who was with her, and he ate it. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And the eyes of both of them were opened, and they knew that they were naked; so they sewed together fig leaves and made coverings for themselves. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_3_7_section">
<h3> God Arraigns Adam and Eve
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then the man and his wife heard the voice of the LORD God walking in the garden in the breeze <span> <a href="#genesis_3_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
of the day, and they hid themselves from the presence of the LORD God among the trees of the garden. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>But the LORD God called out to the man, “Where are you?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>“I heard Your voice in the garden,” he replied, “and I was afraid because I was naked; so I hid myself.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>“Who told you that you were naked?” asked the LORD God. “Have you eaten from the tree of which I commanded you not to eat?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>And the man answered, “The woman whom You gave me, she gave me fruit from the tree, and I ate it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD God said to the woman, “What is this you have done?” 
“The serpent deceived me,” she replied, “and I ate.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_3_13_section">
<h3> The Fate of the Serpent
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So the LORD God said to the serpent: 
“Because you have done this, 
cursed are you above all livestock 
and every beast of the field! 
On your belly will you go, 
and dust you will eat, 
all the days of your life. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And I will put enmity between you and the woman, 
and between your seed and her seed. 
He will crush your head, 
and you will strike his heel.<span> <a href="#genesis_3_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_3_15_section">
<h3> The Punishment of Mankind
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>To the woman He said: 
“I will sharply increase your pain in childbirth; 
in pain you will bring forth children. 
Your desire will be for your husband,<span> <a href="#genesis_3_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

and he will rule over you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>And to Adam He said: 
“Because you have listened to the voice of your wife 
and have eaten from the tree 
of which I commanded you not to eat, 
cursed is the ground because of you; 
through toil you will eat of it 
all the days of your life. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Both thorns and thistles it will yield for you, 
and you will eat the plants of the field. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>By the sweat of your brow 
you will eat your bread, 
until you return to the ground—
because out of it were you taken. 
For dust you are, 
and to dust you shall return.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>And Adam named his wife Eve,<span> <a href="#genesis_3_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
because she would be the mother of all the living. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_3_20_section">
<h3> The Expulsion from Paradise
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>And the LORD God made garments of skin for Adam and his wife, and He clothed them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD God said, “Behold, the man has become like one of Us, knowing good and evil. And now, lest he reach out his hand and take also from the tree of life, and eat, and live forever...” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Therefore the LORD God banished him from the Garden of Eden to work the ground from which he had been taken. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_3_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>So He drove out the man and stationed cherubim on the east side of the Garden of Eden, along with a whirling sword of flame to guard the way to the tree of life. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_3_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_3_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>3:1</em>: Hebrew nachash, translated in this chapter as serpent, is translated in most cases as snake. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_3_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>3:8</em>: Or at the breezy (time); Hebrew unto the Ruach </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_3_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>3:15</em>: Or He will bruise your head, and you will bruise his heel. The same Hebrew root for crush, bruise, or strike appears twice in this verse. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_3_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>3:16</em>: Or You will desire to control your husband </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_3_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>3:20</em>: Eve sounds like the Hebrew for giving life or living. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_4"> 4 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_4_front">
<h3> Cain and Abel
 </h3>
<span> (Hebrews 11:4)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>And Adam had relations with his wife Eve, and she conceived and gave birth to Cain.<span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

“With the help of the LORD I have brought forth a man,” she said. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Later she gave birth to Cain’s brother Abel. 
Now Abel was a keeper of sheep, while Cain was a tiller of the soil. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>So in the course of time, Cain brought some of the fruit of the soil as an offering to the LORD, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>while Abel brought the best portions of the firstborn of his flock. 
And the LORD looked with favor on Abel and his offering, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>but He had no regard for Cain and his offering. So Cain became very angry, and his countenance fell. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>“Why are you angry,” said the LORD to Cain, “and why has your countenance fallen? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>If you do what is right, will you not be accepted? But if you refuse to do what is right, sin is crouching at your door; it desires you,<span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
but you must master it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then Cain said to his brother Abel, “Let us go out to the field.” <span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
And while they were in the field, Cain rose up against his brother Abel and killed him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>And the LORD said to Cain, “Where is your brother Abel?” 
“I do not know!” he answered. “Am I my brother’s keeper?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>“What have you done?” replied the LORD. “The voice of your brother’s blood cries out to Me from the ground. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Now you are cursed and banished from the ground, which has opened its mouth to receive your brother’s blood from your hand. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>When you till the ground, it will no longer yield its produce to you. You will be a fugitive and a wanderer on the earth.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>But Cain said to the LORD, “My punishment <span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
is greater than I can bear. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Behold, this day You have driven me from the face of the earth, and from Your face I will be hidden; I will be a fugitive and a wanderer on the earth, and whoever finds me will kill me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>“Not so!”<span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
replied the LORD. “If anyone slays Cain, then Cain will be avenged sevenfold.” And the LORD placed a mark on Cain, so that no one who found him would kill him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>So Cain went out from the presence of the LORD and settled in the land of Nod,<span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
east of Eden. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_4_16_section">
<h3> The Descendants of Cain
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>And Cain had relations with his wife, and she conceived and gave birth to Enoch. Then Cain built a city and named it after his son Enoch. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Now to Enoch was born Irad, and Irad was the father of Mehujael, and Mehujael was the father of Methusael, and Methusael was the father of Lamech. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>And Lamech married two women, one named Adah and the other Zillah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Adah gave birth to Jabal; he was the father of those who dwell in tents and raise livestock. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>And his brother’s name was Jubal; he was the father of all who play the harp and flute. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And Zillah gave birth to Tubal-cain, a forger of every implement of bronze and iron. And the sister of Tubal-cain was Naamah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Then Lamech said to his wives: 
“Adah and Zillah, hear my voice; 
wives of Lamech, listen to my speech. 
For I have slain a man for wounding me, 
a young man for striking me. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>If Cain is avenged sevenfold, 
then Lamech seventy-sevenfold.”<span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_4_24_section">
<h3> Seth and Enosh
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>And Adam again had relations with his wife, and she gave birth to a son and named him Seth,<span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_8">[8]</a> </span>
saying, “God has granted me another seed in place of Abel, since Cain killed him.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_4_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>And to Seth also a son was born, and he called him Enosh. 
At that time men began to call upon <span> <a href="#genesis_4_footnote_9">[9]</a> </span>
the name of the LORD. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_4_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>4:1</em>: Cain sounds like the Hebrew for acquired or brought forth. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>4:7</em>: Or it desires to control you </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>4:8</em>: SP, LXX, Syriac, and Vulgate; Hebrew Then Cain spoke to his brother Abel. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>4:13</em>: Or guilt or sin </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>4:15</em>: LXX, Vulgate, and Syriac; Hebrew “Very well!” or “Therefore:” </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>4:16</em>: Nod means wandering. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>4:24</em>: Hebrew; LXX can be translated as either seventy times seven or seventy-sevenfold; see also Matthew 18:22. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_8>[8]</a> <em>4:25</em>: Seth probably means granted or appointed. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_4_footnote_9>[9]</a> <em>4:26</em>: Or to proclaim; some translators to invoke or to call themselves by or to profane </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_5"> 5 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_5_front">
<h3> The Descendants of Adam
 </h3>
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:1–3)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>This is the book of the generations of Adam. In the day that God created man, He made him in His own likeness. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Male and female He created them,<span> <a href="#genesis_5_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
 and He blessed them. And in the day they were created, He called them “man.”<span> <a href="#genesis_5_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>When Adam was 130 years old, he had a son in his own likeness, after his own image; and he named him Seth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Seth, Adam lived 800 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>So Adam lived a total of 930 years, and then he died. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>When Seth was 105 years old, he became the father of Enosh. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Enosh, Seth lived 807 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>So Seth lived a total of 912 years, and then he died. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>When Enosh was 90 years old, he became the father of Kenan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Kenan, Enosh lived 815 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>So Enosh lived a total of 905 years, and then he died. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>When Kenan was 70 years old, he became the father of Mahalalel. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Mahalalel, Kenan lived 840 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So Kenan lived a total of 910 years, and then he died. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>When Mahalalel was 65 years old, he became the father of Jared. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Jared, Mahalalel lived 830 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>So Mahalalel lived a total of 895 years, and then he died. 
<span> (Hebrews 11:5)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_5_17_section">
<h3> God Takes Up Enoch
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>When Jared was 162 years old, he became the father of Enoch. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Enoch, Jared lived 800 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>So Jared lived a total of 962 years, and then he died. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>When Enoch was 65 years old, he became the father of Methuselah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Methuselah, Enoch walked with God <span> <a href="#genesis_5_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
300 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>So Enoch lived a total of 365 years. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Enoch walked with God, and then he was no more, because God had taken him away.<span> <a href="#genesis_5_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_5_24_section">
<h3> From Methuselah to Noah
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>When Methuselah was 187 years old, he became the father of Lamech. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Lamech, Methuselah lived 782 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>So Methuselah lived a total of 969 years, and then he died. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>When Lamech was 182 years old, he had a son. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>And he named him Noah,<span> <a href="#genesis_5_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
saying, “May this one comfort us in the labor and toil of our hands caused by the ground that the LORD has cursed.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Noah, Lamech lived 595 years and had other sons and daughters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>So Lamech lived a total of 777 years, and then he died. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_5_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>After Noah was 500 years old, he became the father of Shem, Ham, and Japheth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_5_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_5_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>5:2</em>: Cited in Matthew 19:4 and Mark 10:6 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_5_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>5:2</em>: Hebrew Adam </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_5_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>5:22</em>: LXX pleased God; also in verse 24 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_5_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>5:24</em>: LXX and he was not found, because God had taken him away; cited in Hebrews 11:5 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_5_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>5:29</em>: Noah sounds like the Hebrew for rest or comfort. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_6"> 6 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_6_front">
<h3> Corruption on the Earth
 </h3>
<span> (Matthew 24:36–51)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now when men began to multiply on the face of the earth and daughters were born to them, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>the sons of God saw that the daughters of men were beautiful, and they took as wives whomever they chose. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>So the LORD said, “My Spirit will not contend with man forever,<span> <a href="#genesis_6_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
for he is mortal; his days shall be 120 years.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>The Nephilim were on the earth in those days—and afterward as well—when the sons of God had relations with the daughters of men. And they bore them children who became the mighty men of old, men of renown. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD saw that the wickedness of man was great upon the earth, and that every inclination of the thoughts of his heart was altogether evil all the time. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>And the LORD regretted that He had made man on the earth, and He was grieved in His heart. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>So the LORD said, “I will blot out man, whom I have created, from the face of the earth—every man and beast and crawling creature and bird of the air—for I am grieved that I have made them.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_6_7_section">
<h3> Noah’s Favor with God
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Noah, however, found favor in the eyes of the LORD. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Noah. Noah was a righteous man, blameless in his generation; Noah walked with God. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>And Noah had three sons: Shem, Ham, and Japheth. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Now the earth was corrupt in the sight of God, and full of violence. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>And God looked upon the earth and saw that it was corrupt; for all living creatures <span> <a href="#genesis_6_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
on the earth had corrupted their ways. 
<span> (Hebrews 11:7)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_6_12_section">
<h3> Preparing the Ark
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Then God said to Noah, “The end of all living creatures has come before Me, because through them the earth is full of violence. Now behold, I will destroy both them and the earth. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Make for yourself an ark of gopher wood;<span> <a href="#genesis_6_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
make rooms in the ark and coat it with pitch inside and out. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And this is how you are to build it: The ark is to be 300 cubits long, 50 cubits wide, and 30 cubits high.<span> <a href="#genesis_6_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>You are to make a roof <span> <a href="#genesis_6_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
 for the ark, finish its walls a cubit from the top,<span> <a href="#genesis_6_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
place a door in the side of the ark, and build lower, middle, and upper decks. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>And behold, I will bring floodwaters upon the earth to destroy every creature under the heavens that has the breath of life. Everything on the earth will perish. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>But I will establish My covenant with you, and you will enter the ark—you and your sons and your wife and your sons’ wives with you. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>And you are to bring two of every living creature into the ark—male and female—to keep them alive with you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Two of every kind of bird and animal and crawling creature will come to you to be kept alive. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>You are also to take for yourself every kind of food that is eaten and gather it as food for yourselves and for the animals.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_6_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>So Noah did everything precisely as God had commanded him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_6_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_6_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>6:3</em>: LXX and Syriac My Spirit will not remain in man forever </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_6_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>6:12</em>: Literally all flesh; similarly in verses 13, 17, and 19 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_6_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>6:14</em>: Gopher is an unknown kind of tree; possibly cypress or cedar. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_6_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>6:15</em>: The ark was approximately 450 feet long, 75 feet wide, and 45 feet high (137.2 meters long, 22.9 meters wide, and 13.7 meters high). </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_6_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>6:16</em>: Or skylight or window </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_6_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>6:16</em>: A cubit is approximately 18 inches or 45.7 centimeters. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_7"> 7 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_7_front">
<h3> The Great Flood
 </h3>
<span> (2 Peter 3:1–7)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD said to Noah, “Go into the ark, you and all your family, because I have found you righteous in this generation. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>You are to take with you seven pairs of <span> <a href="#genesis_7_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
every kind of clean animal, a male and its mate; a pair of every kind of unclean animal, a male and its mate; 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>and seven pairs of every kind of bird of the air, male and female, to preserve their offspring on the face of all the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>For seven days from now I will send rain on the earth for forty days and forty nights, and I will wipe from the face of the earth every living thing I have made.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And Noah did all that the LORD had commanded him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Now Noah was 600 years old when the floodwaters came upon the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And Noah and his wife, with his sons and their wives, entered the ark to escape the waters of the flood. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>The clean and unclean animals, the birds, and everything that crawls along the ground 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>came to Noah to enter the ark, two by two, male and female, as God had commanded Noah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>And after seven days the floodwaters came upon the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>In the six hundredth year of Noah’s life, on the seventeenth day of the second month, all the fountains of the great deep burst forth, and the floodgates of the heavens were opened. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>And the rain fell upon the earth for forty days and forty nights. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>On that very day Noah entered the ark, along with his sons Shem, Ham, and Japheth, and his wife, and the three wives of his sons—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>they and every kind of wild animal, livestock, crawling creature, bird, and winged creature. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>They came to Noah to enter the ark, two by two of every creature <span> <a href="#genesis_7_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
with the breath of life. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>And they entered, the male and female of every living thing, as God had commanded Noah. Then the LORD shut him in. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>For forty days the flood kept coming on the earth, and the waters rose and lifted the ark high above the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>So the waters continued to surge and rise greatly on the earth, and the ark floated on the surface of the waters. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Finally, the waters completely prevailed upon the earth, so that all the high mountains under all the heavens were covered. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>The waters rose and covered the mountaintops to a depth of fifteen cubits.<span> <a href="#genesis_7_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>And every living thing that moved upon the earth perished—birds, livestock, animals, every creature that swarms upon the earth, and all mankind. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Of all that was on dry land, everything that had the breath of life in its nostrils died. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>And every living thing on the face of the earth was destroyed—man and livestock, crawling creatures and birds of the air; they were blotted out from the earth, and only Noah and those with him in the ark remained. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_7_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>And the waters prevailed upon the earth for 150 days. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_7_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_7_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>7:2</em>: Or by sevens; also in verse 3 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_7_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>7:15</em>: Literally of all flesh; similarly in verses 16 and 21 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_7_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>7:20</em>: 15 cubits is approximately 22.5 feet or 6.9 meters. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_8"> 8 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_8_front">
<h3> The Ark Rests on Ararat
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>But God remembered Noah and all the animals and livestock that were with him in the ark. And God sent a wind over the earth, and the waters began to subside. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>The springs of the deep and the floodgates of the heavens were closed, and the rain from the sky was restrained. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>The waters receded steadily from the earth, and after 150 days the waters had gone down. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>On the seventeenth day of the seventh month, the ark came to rest on the mountains of Ararat. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And the waters continued to recede until the tenth month, and on the first day of the tenth month the tops of the mountains became visible. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_8_5_section">
<h3> Noah Sends a Raven and a Dove
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>After forty days Noah opened the window he had made in the ark 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>and sent out a raven. It kept flying back and forth until the waters had dried up from the earth. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then Noah sent out <span> <a href="#genesis_8_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
a dove to see if the waters had receded from the surface of the ground. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>But the dove found no place to rest her foot, and she returned to him in the ark, because the waters were still covering the surface of all the earth. So he reached out his hand and brought her back inside the ark. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Noah waited seven more days and again sent out the dove from the ark. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And behold, the dove returned to him in the evening with a freshly plucked olive leaf in her beak. So Noah knew that the waters had receded from the earth. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>And Noah waited seven more days and sent out the dove again, but this time she did not return to him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_8_12_section">
<h3> Exiting the Ark
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>In Noah’s six hundred and first year, on the first day of the first month, the waters had dried up from the earth. So Noah removed the covering from the ark and saw that the surface of the ground was dry. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>By the twenty-seventh day of the second month, the earth was fully dry. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Then God said to Noah, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>“Come out of the ark, you and your wife, along with your sons and their wives. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Bring out all the living creatures that are with you—birds, livestock, and everything that crawls upon the ground—so that they can spread out over the earth and be fruitful and multiply upon it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>So Noah came out, along with his sons and his wife and his sons’ wives. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Every living creature, every creeping thing, and every bird—everything that moves upon the earth—came out of the ark, kind by kind. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_8_19_section">
<h3> Noah Builds an Altar
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Then Noah built an altar to the LORD. And taking from every kind of clean animal and clean bird, he offered burnt offerings on the altar. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>When the LORD smelled the pleasing aroma, He said in His heart, “Never again will I curse the ground because of man, even though every inclination of his heart is evil from his youth. And never again will I destroy all living creatures as I have done. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_8_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>As long as the earth endures, 
seedtime and harvest, 
cold and heat, 
summer and winter, 
day and night 
shall never cease.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_8_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_8_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>8:8</em>: Literally sent out from him or sent out from it </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_9"> 9 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_9_front">
<h3> The Covenant of the Rainbow
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>And God blessed Noah and his sons and said to them, “Be fruitful and multiply and fill the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>The fear and dread of you will fall on every living creature on the earth, every bird of the air, every creature that crawls on the ground, and all the fish of the sea. They are delivered into your hand. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Everything that lives and moves will be food for you; just as I gave you the green plants, I now give you all things. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>But you must not eat meat with its lifeblood still in it. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And surely I will require the life of any man or beast by whose hand your lifeblood is shed. I will demand an accounting from anyone who takes the life of his fellow man: 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Whoever sheds the blood of man, 
by man his blood will be shed; 
for in His own image 
God has made mankind. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>But as for you, 
be fruitful and multiply; 
spread out across the earth 
and multiply upon it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then God said to Noah and his sons with him, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>“Behold, I now establish My covenant with you and your descendants after you, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>and with every living creature that was with you—the birds, the livestock, and every beast of the earth—every living thing that came out of the ark. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And I establish My covenant with you: Never again will all life be cut off by the waters of a flood; never again will there be a flood to destroy the earth.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>And God said, “This is the sign of the covenant I am making between Me and you and every living creature with you, a covenant for all generations to come: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>I have set My rainbow in the clouds, and it will be a sign of the covenant between Me and the earth. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Whenever I form clouds over the earth and the rainbow appears in the clouds, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>I will remember My covenant between Me and you and every living creature of every kind. Never again will the waters become a flood to destroy all life. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>And whenever the rainbow appears in the clouds, I will see it and remember the everlasting covenant between God and every living creature of every kind that is on the earth.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>So God said to Noah, “This is the sign of the covenant that I have established between Me and every creature on the earth.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_9_17_section">
<h3> Noah’s Shame and Canaan’s Curse
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>The sons of Noah who came out of the ark were Shem, Ham, and Japheth. And Ham was the father of Canaan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>These three were the sons of Noah, and from them the whole earth was populated. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Now Noah, a man of the soil, proceeded <span> <a href="#genesis_9_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
to plant a vineyard. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>But when he drank some of its wine, he became drunk and uncovered himself inside his tent. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And Ham, the father of Canaan, saw his father’s nakedness and told his two brothers outside. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Then Shem and Japheth took a garment and placed it across their shoulders, and walking backward, they covered their father’s nakedness. Their faces were turned away so that they did not see their father’s nakedness. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>When Noah awoke from his drunkenness and learned what his youngest son had done to him, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>he said, 
“Cursed be Canaan! 
A servant of servants 
shall he be to his brothers.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_9_25_section">
<h3> Shem’s Blessing and Noah’s Death
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>He also declared: 
“Blessed be the LORD, the God of Shem! 
May Canaan be the servant of Shem. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>May God expand the territory of Japheth;<span> <a href="#genesis_9_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>

may he dwell in the tents of Shem, 
and may Canaan be his servant.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>After the flood, Noah lived 350 years. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_9_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>So Noah lived a total of 950 years, and then he died. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_9_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_9_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>9:20</em>: Or was the first </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_9_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>9:27</em>: Japheth sounds like the Hebrew for expand. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_10"> 10 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_10_front">
<h3> The Table of Nations
 </h3>
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:4–27)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Noah’s sons Shem, Ham, and Japheth, who also had sons after the flood. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>The sons of Japheth: 
Gomer, Magog, Madai, Javan, Tubal, Meshech, and Tiras. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>The sons of Gomer: 
Ashkenaz, Riphath, and Togarmah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>And the sons of Javan: 
Elishah, Tarshish, the Kittites, and the Rodanites.<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>From these, the maritime peoples separated into their territories, according to their languages, by clans within their nations. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>The sons of Ham: 
Cush, Mizraim, Put, and Canaan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>The sons of Cush: 
Seba, Havilah, Sabtah,<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
Raamah, and Sabteca. 
And the sons of Raamah: 
Sheba and Dedan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Cush was the father of Nimrod, who began to be a mighty one <span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
on the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>He was a mighty hunter before <span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
the LORD; so it is said, “Like Nimrod, a mighty hunter before the LORD.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>His kingdom began in Babylon, Erech, Accad, and Calneh, in the land of Shinar.<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>From that land he went forth into Assyria, where he built Nineveh, Rehoboth-Ir, Calah, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>and Resen, which is between Nineveh and the great city of Calah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Mizraim was the father of the Ludites, the Anamites, the Lehabites, the Naphtuhites, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>the Pathrusites, the Casluhites (from whom the Philistines came), and the Caphtorites.<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And Canaan was the father of Sidon his firstborn,<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>
and of the Hittites, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>the Jebusites, the Amorites, the Girgashites, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>the Hivites, the Arkites, the Sinites, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>the Arvadites, the Zemarites, and the Hamathites. 
Later the Canaanite clans were scattered, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>and the borders of Canaan extended from Sidon toward Gerar as far as Gaza, and then toward Sodom, Gomorrah, Admah, and Zeboiim, as far as Lasha. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Ham according to their clans, languages, lands, and nations. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>And sons were also born to Shem, the older brother of Japheth;<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_8">[8]</a> </span>
Shem was the forefather of all the sons of Eber. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>The sons of Shem: 
Elam, Asshur, Arphaxad, Lud, and Aram. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>The sons of Aram: 
Uz, Hul, Gether, and Mash.<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_9">[9]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Arphaxad was the father of Shelah,<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_10">[10]</a> </span>
and Shelah was the father of Eber. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Two sons were born to Eber: One was named Peleg,<span> <a href="#genesis_10_footnote_11">[11]</a> </span>
because in his days the earth was divided, and his brother was named Joktan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>And Joktan was the father of Almodad, Sheleph, Hazarmaveth, Jerah, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Hadoram, Uzal, Diklah, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>Obal, Abimael, Sheba, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Ophir, Havilah, and Jobab. All these were sons of Joktan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Their territory extended from Mesha to Sephar, in the eastern hill country. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Shem, according to their clans, languages, lands, and nations. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_10_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>All these are the clans of Noah’s sons, according to their generations and nations. From these the nations of the earth spread out after the flood. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_10_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>10:4</em>: SP and some MT manuscripts (see also LXX and 1 Chronicles 1:7); most MT manuscripts Dodanites </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>10:7</em>: Sabtah is a variant of Sabta; see 1 Chronicles 1:9. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>10:8</em>: Or who established himself as a mighty warrior or who became the first fearless leader </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>10:9</em>: Or in defiance of; twice in this verse </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>10:10</em>: That is, Babylonia </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>10:14</em>: Some translators adjust the Hebrew word order to the Casluhites, and the Caphtorites (from whom the Philistines came); see also Jeremiah 47:4 and Amos 9:7. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>10:15</em>: Or of the Sidonians, the foremost </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_8>[8]</a> <em>10:21</em>: Or Shem, whose older brother was Japheth </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_9>[9]</a> <em>10:23</em>: Hebrew; LXX and 1 Chronicles 1:17 Meshech </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_10>[10]</a> <em>10:24</em>: Hebrew; LXX (see also Luke 3:35–36) And Arphaxad was the father of Cainan, and Cainan was the father of Shelah, </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_10_footnote_11>[11]</a> <em>10:25</em>: Peleg means division. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_11"> 11 </h2>
