<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_11_front">
<h3> The Tower of Babel
 </h3>
<span> (Deuteronomy 32:8; Acts 2:1–13)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now the whole world had one language and a common form of speech. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And as people journeyed eastward,<span> <a href="#genesis_11_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
 they found a plain in the land of Shinar <span> <a href="#genesis_11_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
and settled there. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>And they said to one another, “Come, let us make bricks and bake them thoroughly.” So they used brick instead of stone, and tar instead of mortar. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>“Come,” they said, “let us build for ourselves a city with a tower that reaches to the heavens, that we may make a name for ourselves and not be scattered over the face of all the earth.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD came down to see the city and the tower that the sons of men were building. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>And the LORD said, “If they have begun to do this as one people speaking the same language, then nothing they devise will be beyond them. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Come, let Us go down and confuse their language, so that they will not understand one another’s speech.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>So the LORD scattered them from there over the face of all the earth, and they stopped building the city. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>That is why it is called Babel,<span> <a href="#genesis_11_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
for there the LORD confused the language of the whole world, and from that place the LORD scattered them over the face of all the earth. 
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:17–27)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_11_9_section">
<h3> Genealogy from Shem to Abram
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Shem. Two years after the flood, when Shem was 100 years old, he became the father of Arphaxad. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Arphaxad, Shem lived 500 years and had other sons and daughters. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>When Arphaxad was 35 years old, he became the father of Shelah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Shelah, Arphaxad lived 403 years and had other sons and daughters.<span> <a href="#genesis_11_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>When Shelah was 30 years old, he became the father of Eber. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Eber, Shelah lived 403 years and had other sons and daughters. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>When Eber was 34 years old, he became the father of Peleg. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Peleg, Eber lived 430 years and had other sons and daughters. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>When Peleg was 30 years old, he became the father of Reu. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Reu, Peleg lived 209 years and had other sons and daughters. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>When Reu was 32 years old, he became the father of Serug. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Serug, Reu lived 207 years and had other sons and daughters. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>When Serug was 30 years old, he became the father of Nahor. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Nahor, Serug lived 200 years and had other sons and daughters. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>When Nahor was 29 years old, he became the father of Terah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>And after he had become the father of Terah, Nahor lived 119 years and had other sons and daughters. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>When Terah was 70 years old, he became the father of Abram, Nahor, and Haran. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_11_26_section">
<h3> Terah’s Descendants
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Terah. Terah became the father of Abram, Nahor, and Haran. And Haran became the father of Lot. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>During his father Terah’s lifetime, Haran died in his native land, in Ur of the Chaldeans. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>And Abram and Nahor took wives for themselves. Abram’s wife was named Sarai, and Nahor’s wife was named Milcah; she was the daughter of Haran, who was the father of both Milcah and Iscah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>But Sarai was barren; she had no children. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>And Terah took his son Abram, his grandson Lot son of Haran, and his daughter-in-law Sarai the wife of Abram, and they set out from Ur of the Chaldeans for the land of Canaan. But when they arrived in Haran, they settled there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_11_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>Terah lived 205 years, and he died in Haran. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_11_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_11_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>11:2</em>: Or from the east or in the east </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_11_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>11:2</em>: That is, Babylonia </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_11_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>11:9</em>: Or Babylon; the Hebrew word for Babel sounds like the Hebrew for confused. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_11_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>11:13</em>: Hebrew; LXX (see also Luke 3:35–36) 12 When Arphaxad was 135 years old, he became the father of Cainan. 13 And after he had become the father of Cainan, Arphaxad lived 430 years and had other sons and daughters, and then he died. When Cainan had lived 130 years, he became the father of Shelah. And after he had become the father of Shelah, Cainan lived 330 years and had other sons and daughters. Note that LXX also adds 100 years to the ages of Shelah, Eber, Peleg, Reu, Serug, and Nahor in this genealogy. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_12"> 12 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_12_front">
<h3> The Call of Abram
 </h3>
<span> (Genesis 26:1–5; Acts 7:1–8)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD said to Abram, “Leave your country, your kindred, and your father’s household, and go to the land I will show you.<span> <a href="#genesis_12_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>I will make you into a great nation, 
and I will bless you; 
I will make your name great, 
so that you will be a blessing. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>I will bless those who bless you 
and curse those who curse you; 
and all the families of the earth 
will be blessed through you.<span> <a href="#genesis_12_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>So Abram departed, as the LORD had directed him, and Lot went with him. Abram was seventy-five years old when he left Haran. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And Abram took his wife Sarai, his nephew Lot, and all the possessions and people they had acquired in Haran, and set out for the land of Canaan. 
When they came to the land of Canaan, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Abram traveled through the land as far as the site of the Oak <span> <a href="#genesis_12_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
of Moreh at Shechem. And at that time the Canaanites were in the land. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD appeared to Abram and said, “I will give this land to your offspring.<span> <a href="#genesis_12_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
” So Abram built an altar there to the LORD, who had appeared to him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>From there Abram moved on to the hill country east of Bethel and pitched his tent, with Bethel to the west and Ai to the east. There he built an altar to the LORD, and he called on the name of the LORD. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>And Abram journeyed on toward the Negev. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_12_9_section">
<h3> Abram and Sarai in Egypt
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Now there was a famine in the land. So Abram went down to Egypt to live there for a while because the famine was severe. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>As he was about to enter Egypt, he said to his wife Sarai, “Look, I know that you are a beautiful woman, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>and when the Egyptians see you, they will say, ‘This is his wife.’ Then they will kill me but will let you live. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Please say you are my sister, so that I will be treated well for your sake, and on account of you my life will be spared.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So when Abram entered Egypt, the Egyptians saw that the woman was very beautiful. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>When Pharaoh’s officials saw Sarai, they commended her to him, and she was taken into the palace of Pharaoh. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>He treated Abram well on her account, and Abram acquired sheep and cattle, male and female donkeys, menservants and maidservants, and camels. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>The LORD, however, afflicted Pharaoh and his household with severe plagues because of Abram’s wife Sarai. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>So Pharaoh summoned Abram and asked, “What have you done to me? Why didn’t you tell me she was your wife? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Why did you say, ‘She is my sister,’ so that I took her as my wife? Now then, here is your wife. Take her and go!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_12_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Then Pharaoh gave his men orders concerning Abram, and they sent him away with his wife and all his possessions. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_12_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_12_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>12:1</em>: Cited in Acts 7:3 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_12_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>12:3</em>: See Galatians 3:8 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_12_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>12:6</em>: Or Terebinth or Great Tree </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_12_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>12:7</em>: Cited in Galatians 3:16 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_13"> 13 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_13_front">
<h3> Abram and Lot Part Ways
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>So Abram went up out of Egypt into the Negev—he and his wife and all his possessions—and Lot was with him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And Abram had become extremely wealthy in livestock and silver and gold. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>From the Negev he journeyed from place to place toward Bethel, until he came to the place between Bethel and Ai where his tent had formerly been pitched, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>to the site where he had built the altar. And there Abram called on the name of the LORD. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Now Lot, who was traveling with Abram, also had flocks and herds and tents. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>But the land was unable to support both of them while they stayed together, for they had so many possessions that they were unable to coexist. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And there was discord between the herdsmen of Abram and the herdsmen of Lot. At that time the Canaanites and the Perizzites were also living in the land. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>So Abram said to Lot, “Please let there be no contention between you and me, or between your herdsmen and my herdsmen. After all, we are kinsmen. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Is not the whole land before you? Now separate yourself from me. If you go to the left, I will go to the right; if you go to the right, I will go to the left.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_13_9_section">
<h3> Lot Proceeds toward Sodom
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>And Lot looked out and saw that the whole plain of the Jordan, all the way to Zoar, was well watered like the garden of the LORD, like the land of Egypt. (This was before the LORD destroyed Sodom and Gomorrah.) 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>So Lot chose the whole plain of the Jordan for himself and set out toward the east. And Abram and Lot parted company. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Abram lived in the land of Canaan, but Lot settled in the cities of the plain and pitched his tent toward Sodom. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>But the men of Sodom were wicked, sinning greatly against the LORD. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_13_13_section">
<h3> God Renews the Promise to Abram
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>After Lot had departed, the LORD said to Abram, “Now lift up your eyes from the place where you are, and look to the north and south and east and west, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>for all the land that you see, I will give to you and your offspring forever.<span> <a href="#genesis_13_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>I will make your offspring like the dust of the earth, so that if one could count the dust of the earth, then your offspring could be counted. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Get up and walk around the land, through its length and breadth, for I will give it to you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_13_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>So Abram moved his tent and went to live near the Oaks <span> <a href="#genesis_13_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
of Mamre at Hebron, where he built an altar to the LORD. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_13_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_13_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>13:15</em>: Cited in Galatians 3:16 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_13_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>13:18</em>: Or Terebinths or Great Trees </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_14"> 14 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_14_front">
<h3> The War of the Kings
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>In those days Amraphel king of Shinar,<span> <a href="#genesis_14_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
Arioch king of Ellasar, Chedorlaomer king of Elam, and Tidal king of Goiim
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>went to war against Bera king of Sodom, Birsha king of Gomorrah, Shinab king of Admah, Shemeber king of Zeboiim, and the king of Bela (that is, Zoar).
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>The latter five came as allies to the Valley of Siddim (that is, the Salt Sea <span> <a href="#genesis_14_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
).
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>For twelve years they had been subject to Chedorlaomer, but in the thirteenth year they rebelled.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>In the fourteenth year, Chedorlaomer and the kings allied with him went out and defeated the Rephaites in Ashteroth-karnaim, the Zuzites in Ham, the Emites in Shaveh-kiriathaim,
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>and the Horites in the area of Mount Seir, as far as El-paran, which is near the desert.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Then they turned back to invade En-mishpat (that is, Kadesh), and they conquered the whole territory of the Amalekites, as well as the Amorites who lived in Hazazon-tamar.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then the king of Sodom, the king of Gomorrah, the king of Admah, the king of Zeboiim, and the king of Bela (that is, Zoar) marched out and arrayed themselves for battle in the Valley of Siddim <span> <a href="#genesis_14_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>against Chedorlaomer king of Elam, Tidal king of Goiim, Amraphel king of Shinar, and Arioch king of Ellasar—four kings against five.
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_14_9_section">
<h3> Abram Rescues Lot
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Now the Valley of Siddim was full of tar pits, and as the kings of Sodom and Gomorrah fled, some men fell into the pits, but the survivors fled to the hill country.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>The four kings seized all the goods of Sodom and Gomorrah and all their food, and they went on their way.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>They also carried off Abram’s nephew Lot and his possessions, since Lot was living in Sodom.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Then an escapee came and reported this to Abram the Hebrew. Now Abram was living near the Oaks <span> <a href="#genesis_14_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
 of Mamre the Amorite, a brother of Eshcol and Aner, all of whom were bound by treaty <span> <a href="#genesis_14_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
to Abram.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>And when Abram heard that his relative had been captured, he mobilized the 318 trained men born in his household, and they set out in pursuit as far as Dan.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>During the night, Abram divided his forces and routed Chedorlaomer’s army, pursuing them as far as Hobah, north of Damascus.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>He retrieved all the goods, as well as his relative Lot and his possessions, together with the women and the rest of the people.
<span> (Psalms 110:1–7; Hebrews 7:1–10)
 </span>
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_14_16_section">
<h3> Melchizedek Blesses Abram
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>After Abram returned from defeating Chedorlaomer and the kings allied with him, the king of Sodom went out to meet him in the Valley of Shaveh (that is, the King’s Valley).
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Then Melchizedek king of Salem brought out bread and wine—since he was priest of God Most High <span> <a href="#genesis_14_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>and he blessed Abram and said:
“Blessed be Abram by God Most High,
Creator of heaven and earth,
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>and blessed be God Most High,
who has delivered your enemies into your hand.”
Then Abram gave Melchizedek a tenth of everything.
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>The king of Sodom said to Abram, “Give me the people, but take the goods for yourself.”
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>But Abram replied to the king of Sodom, “I have raised my hand to the LORD God Most High, Creator of heaven and earth,
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>that I will not accept even a thread, or a strap of a sandal, or anything that belongs to you, lest you should say, ‘I have made Abram rich.’
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_14_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>I will accept nothing but what my men have eaten and the share for the men who went with me—Aner, Eshcol, and Mamre. They may take their portion.”
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_14_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_14_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>14:1</em>: That is, Babylonia; also in verse 9 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_14_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>14:3</em>: That is, the Dead Sea </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_14_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>14:8</em>: That is, the Valley of the Dead Sea </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_14_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>14:13</em>: Or Terebinths or Great Trees </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_14_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>14:13</em>: Forms of the Hebrew berit are translated in most passages as covenant. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_14_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>14:18</em>: Hebrew El-Elyon; also in verses 19, 20, and 22; cited in Hebrews 7:1 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_15"> 15 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_15_front">
<h3> God’s Covenant with Abram
 </h3>
<span> (Romans 4:1–12; Hebrews 11:8–19)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>After these events, the word of the LORD came to Abram in a vision: 
“Do not be afraid, Abram. 
I am your shield, 
your very great reward.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>But Abram replied, “O Lord GOD, what can You give me, since I remain childless, and the heir of my house is Eliezer of Damascus?” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Abram continued, “Behold, You have given me no offspring, so a servant in my household will be my heir.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Then the word of the LORD came to Abram, saying, “This one will not be your heir, but one who comes from your own body will be your heir.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And the LORD took him outside and said, “Now look to the heavens and count the stars, if you are able.” Then He told him, “So shall your offspring be.”<span> <a href="#genesis_15_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Abram believed the LORD, and it was credited to him as righteousness.<span> <a href="#genesis_15_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>The LORD also told him, “I am the LORD, who brought you out of Ur of the Chaldeans to give you this land to possess.” 
<span> (Numbers 34:1–15; Romans 4:13–25)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_15_7_section">
<h3> God Confirms His Promise
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>But Abram replied, “Lord GOD, how can I know that I will possess it?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>And the LORD said to him, “Bring Me a heifer, a goat, and a ram, each three years old, along with a turtledove and a young pigeon.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>So Abram brought all these to Him, split each of them down the middle, and laid the halves opposite each other. The birds, however, he did not cut in half. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And the birds of prey descended on the carcasses, but Abram drove them away. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>As the sun was setting, Abram fell into a deep sleep, and suddenly great terror and darkness overwhelmed him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD said to Abram, “Know for certain that your descendants will be strangers in a land that is not their own, and they will be enslaved and mistreated four hundred years. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>But I will judge the nation they serve as slaves, and afterward they will depart with many possessions.<span> <a href="#genesis_15_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>You, however, will go to your fathers in peace and be buried at a ripe old age. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>In the fourth generation your descendants will return here, for the iniquity of the Amorites is not yet complete.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>When the sun had set and darkness had fallen, behold, a smoking firepot and a flaming torch appeared and passed between the halves of the carcasses. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>On that day the LORD made a covenant with Abram, saying, “To your descendants I have given this land—from the river of Egypt to the great River Euphrates—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>the land of the Kenites, Kenizzites, Kadmonites, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Hittites, Perizzites, Rephaites, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_15_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Amorites, Canaanites, Girgashites, and Jebusites.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_15_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_15_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>15:5</em>: Cited in Romans 4:18 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_15_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>15:6</em>: Cited in Romans 4:3, Romans 4:22, Galatians 3:6, and James 2:23 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_15_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>15:14</em>: Cited in Acts 7:6–7 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_16"> 16 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_16_front">
<h3> Hagar and Ishmael
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Abram’s wife Sarai had borne him no children, but she had an Egyptian maidservant named Hagar. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>So Sarai said to Abram, “Look now, the LORD has prevented me from bearing children. Please go to my maidservant; perhaps I can build a family by her.” 
And Abram listened to the voice of Sarai. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>So after he had lived in Canaan for ten years, his wife Sarai took her Egyptian maidservant Hagar and gave her to Abram to be his wife. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>And he slept with Hagar, and she conceived. But when Hagar realized that she was pregnant, she began to despise her mistress.<span> <a href="#genesis_16_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Then Sarai said to Abram, “May the wrong done to me be upon you! I delivered my servant into your arms, and ever since she saw that she was pregnant, she has treated me with contempt. May the LORD judge between you and me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>“Here,” said Abram, “your servant is in your hands. Do whatever you want with her.” Then Sarai treated Hagar so harshly that she fled from her. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Now the angel <span> <a href="#genesis_16_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
of the LORD found Hagar by a spring of water in the desert—the spring along the road to Shur. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>“Hagar, servant of Sarai,” he said, “where have you come from, and where are you going?” 
“I am running away from my mistress Sarai,” she replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>So the angel of the LORD told her, “Return to your mistress and submit to her authority.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Then the angel added, “I will greatly multiply your offspring so that they will be too numerous to count.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>The angel of the LORD proceeded: 
“Behold, you have conceived and will bear a son. 
And you shall name him Ishmael,<span> <a href="#genesis_16_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

for the LORD has heard your cry of affliction. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>He will be a wild donkey of a man, 
and his hand will be against everyone, 
and everyone’s hand against him; 
he will live in hostility 
toward all his brothers.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>So Hagar gave this name to the LORD who had spoken to her: “You are the God who sees me,<span> <a href="#genesis_16_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
” for she said, “Here I have seen the One who sees me!” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Therefore the well was called Beer-lahai-roi.<span> <a href="#genesis_16_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
It is located between Kadesh and Bered. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And Hagar bore Abram a son, and Abram gave the name Ishmael to the son she had borne. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_16_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Abram was eighty-six years old when Hagar bore Ishmael to him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_16_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_16_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>16:4</em>: Or her mistress became despised in her sight </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_16_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>16:7</em>: Or Angel; also in verses 9, 10, and 11; corresponding pronouns may also be capitalized. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_16_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>16:11</em>: Ishmael means God hears. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_16_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>16:13</em>: Hebrew El-Roi </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_16_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>16:14</em>: Beer-lahai-roi means well of the Living One who sees me. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_17"> 17 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_17_front">
<h3> Abraham to Father Many Nations
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>When Abram was ninety-nine years old, the LORD appeared to him and said, “I am God Almighty.<span> <a href="#genesis_17_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
Walk before Me and be blameless. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>I will establish My covenant between Me and you, and I will multiply you exceedingly.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Then Abram fell facedown, and God said to him, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>“As for Me, this is My covenant with you: You will be the father of many nations. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>No longer will you be called Abram,<span> <a href="#genesis_17_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
 but your name will be Abraham,<span> <a href="#genesis_17_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
 for I have made you a father of many nations.<span> <a href="#genesis_17_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>I will make you exceedingly fruitful; I will make nations of you, and kings will descend from you. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>I will establish My covenant as an everlasting covenant between Me and you and your descendants after you, to be your God and the God of your descendants after you. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>And to you and your descendants I will give the land where you are residing—all the land of Canaan—as an eternal possession; and I will be their God.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_17_8_section">
<h3> The Covenant of Circumcision
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>God also said to Abraham, “You must keep My covenant—you and your descendants in the generations after you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>This is My covenant with you and your descendants after you, which you are to keep: Every male among you must be circumcised. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>You are to circumcise the flesh of your foreskin, and this will be a sign of the covenant between Me and you. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Generation after generation, every male must be circumcised when he is eight days old, including those born in your household and those purchased from a foreigner—even those who are not your offspring. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Whether they are born in your household or purchased, they must be circumcised. My covenant in your flesh will be an everlasting covenant. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>But if any male is not circumcised, he will be cut off from his people; he has broken My covenant.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Then God said to Abraham, “As for Sarai your wife, do not call her Sarai, for her name is to be Sarah.<span> <a href="#genesis_17_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>And I will bless her and will surely give you a son by her. I will bless her, and she will be the mother of nations; kings of peoples will descend from her.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Abraham fell facedown. Then he laughed and said to himself, “Can a child be born to a man who is a hundred years old? Can Sarah give birth at the age of ninety?” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>And Abraham said to God, “O that Ishmael might live under Your blessing!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>But God replied, “Your wife Sarah will indeed bear you a son, and you are to name him Isaac.<span> <a href="#genesis_17_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
I will establish My covenant with him as an everlasting covenant for his descendants after him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>As for Ishmael, I have heard you, and I will surely bless him; I will make him fruitful and multiply him greatly. He will become the father of twelve rulers, and I will make him into a great nation. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>But I will establish My covenant with Isaac, whom Sarah will bear to you at this time next year.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>When He had finished speaking with Abraham, God went up from him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>On that very day Abraham took his son Ishmael and all those born in his household or purchased with his money—every male among the members of Abraham’s household—and he circumcised them, just as God had told him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>So Abraham was ninety-nine years old when he was circumcised, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>and his son Ishmael was thirteen; 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Abraham and his son Ishmael were circumcised on the same day. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_17_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>And all the men of Abraham’s household—both servants born in his household and those purchased from foreigners—were circumcised with him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_17_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_17_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>17:1</em>: Hebrew El-Shaddai </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_17_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>17:5</em>: Abram means exalted father. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_17_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>17:5</em>: Abraham means father of many. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_17_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>17:5</em>: Cited in Romans 4:17 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_17_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>17:15</em>: Both Sarai and Sarah mean princess; the change in spelling may reflect the difference in dialect between Ur and Canaan. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_17_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>17:19</em>: Isaac means he laughs. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_18"> 18 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_18_front">
<h3> The Three Visitors
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD appeared to Abraham by the Oaks <span> <a href="#genesis_18_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
of Mamre in the heat of the day, while he was sitting at the entrance of his tent. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And Abraham looked up and saw three men standing nearby. When he saw them, he ran from the entrance of his tent to meet them and bowed low to the ground. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>“My lord,” said Abraham, “if I have found favor in your sight, please do not pass your servant by. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Let a little water be brought, that you may wash your feet and rest yourselves under the tree. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And I will bring a bit of bread so that you may refresh yourselves. This is why you have passed your servant’s way. After that, you may continue on your way.” 
“Yes,” they replied, “you may do as you have said.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>So Abraham hurried into the tent and said to Sarah, “Quick! Prepare three seahs of fine flour,<span> <a href="#genesis_18_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
knead it, and bake some bread.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Meanwhile, Abraham ran to the herd, selected a tender and choice calf, and gave it to a servant, who hurried to prepare it. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham brought curds and milk and the calf that had been prepared, and he set them before the men and stood by them under the tree as they ate. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_18_8_section">
<h3> Sarah Laughs at the Promise
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>“Where is your wife Sarah?” they asked. 
“There, in the tent,” he replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD said, “I will surely return to you at this time next year, and your wife Sarah will have a son!” 
Now Sarah was behind him, listening at the entrance to the tent. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And Abraham and Sarah were already old and well along in years; Sarah had passed the age of childbearing. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>So she laughed to herself, saying, “After I am worn out and my master is old, will I now have this pleasure?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>And the LORD asked Abraham, “Why did Sarah laugh and say, ‘Can I really bear a child when I am old?’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Is anything too difficult for the LORD? At the appointed time I will return to you—in about a year—and Sarah will have a son.”<span> <a href="#genesis_18_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>But Sarah was afraid, so she denied it and said, “I did not laugh.” 
“No,” replied the LORD, “but you did laugh.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_18_15_section">
<h3> Abraham Intercedes for Sodom
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>When the men got up to leave, they looked out over Sodom, and Abraham walked along with them to see them off. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>And the LORD said, “Shall I hide from Abraham what I am about to do? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Abraham will surely become a great and powerful nation, and through him all the nations of the earth will be blessed. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>For I have chosen him, so that he will command his children and his household after him to keep the way of the LORD by doing what is right and just, in order that the LORD may bring upon Abraham what He has promised.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD said, “The outcry against Sodom and Gomorrah is great. Because their sin is so grievous, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>I will go down to see if their actions fully justify the outcry that has reached Me. If not, I will find out.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And the two men turned away and went toward Sodom, but Abraham remained standing before the LORD. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Abraham stepped forward and said, “Will You really sweep away the righteous with the wicked? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>What if there are fifty righteous ones in the city? Will You really sweep it away and not spare the place for the sake of the fifty righteous ones who are there? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Far be it from You to do such a thing—to kill the righteous with the wicked, so that the righteous and the wicked are treated alike. Far be it from You! Will not the Judge of all the earth do what is right?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>So the LORD replied, “If I find fifty righteous ones within the city of Sodom, on their account I will spare the whole place.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham answered, “Now that I have ventured to speak to the Lord—though I am but dust and ashes—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>suppose the fifty righteous ones lack five. Will You destroy the whole city for the lack of five?” 
He replied, “If I find forty-five there, I will not destroy it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Once again Abraham spoke to the LORD, “Suppose forty are found there?” 
He answered, “On account of the forty, I will not do it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham said, “May the Lord not be angry, but let me speak further. Suppose thirty are found there?” 
He replied, “If I find thirty there, I will not do it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>And Abraham said, “Now that I have ventured to speak to the Lord, suppose twenty are found there?” 
He answered, “On account of the twenty, I will not destroy it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>Finally, Abraham said, “May the Lord not be angry, but let me speak once more. Suppose ten are found there?” 
And He answered, “On account of the ten, I will not destroy it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_18_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>When the LORD had finished speaking with Abraham, He departed, and Abraham returned home. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_18_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_18_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>18:1</em>: Or Terebinths or Great Trees </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_18_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>18:6</em>: 3 seahs is approximately 19.8 dry quarts or 21.9 liters (probably about 24.5 pounds or 11.1 kilograms of flour). </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_18_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>18:14</em>: Cited in Romans 9:9 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_19"> 19 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_19_front">
<h3> Lot Welcomes the Angels
 </h3>
<span> (Judges 19:1–30)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now the two angels arrived at Sodom in the evening, and Lot was sitting in the gateway of the city. When Lot saw them, he got up to meet them, bowed facedown, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>and said, “My lords, please turn aside into the house of your servant; wash your feet and spend the night. Then you can rise early and go on your way.” 
“No,” they answered, “we will spend the night in the square.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>But Lot insisted so strongly that they followed him into his house. He prepared a feast for them and baked unleavened bread, and they ate. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Before they had gone to bed, all the men of the city of Sodom, both young and old, surrounded the house. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>They called out to Lot, saying, “Where are the men who came to you tonight? Send them out to us so we can have relations with them!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Lot went outside to meet them, shutting the door behind him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>“Please, my brothers,” he pleaded, “don’t do such a wicked thing! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Look, I have two daughters who have never slept with a man. Let me bring them to you, and you can do to them as you please. But do not do anything to these men, for they have come under the protection of my roof.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>“Get out of the way!” they replied. And they declared, “This one came here as a foreigner, and he is already acting like a judge! Now we will treat you worse than them.” And they pressed in on Lot and moved in to break down the door. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>But the men inside reached out, pulled Lot into the house with them, and shut the door. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And they struck the men at the entrance, young and old, with blindness, so that they wearied themselves trying to find the door. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_19_11_section">
<h3> Lot Flees to Zoar
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Then the two men said to Lot, “Do you have anyone else here—a son-in-law, your sons or daughters, or anyone else in the city who belongs to you? Get them out of here, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>because we are about to destroy this place. For the outcry to the LORD against its people is so great that He has sent us to destroy it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So Lot went out and spoke to the sons-in-law who were pledged in marriage to his daughters. “Get up,” he said. “Get out of this place, for the LORD is about to destroy the city!” But his sons-in-law thought he was joking. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>At daybreak the angels hurried Lot along, saying, “Get up! Take your wife and your two daughters who are here, or you will be swept away in the punishment of the city.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>But when Lot hesitated, the men grabbed his hand and the hands of his wife and his two daughters. And they led them safely out of the city, because of the LORD’s compassion for them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>As soon as the men had brought them out, one of them said, “Run for your lives! Do not look back, and do not stop anywhere on the plain! Flee to the mountains, or you will be swept away!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>But Lot replied, “No, my lords, please! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Your servant has indeed found favor in your sight, and you have shown me great kindness by sparing my life. But I cannot run to the mountains; the disaster will overtake me, and I will die. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Look, there is a town nearby where I can flee, and it is a small place. Please let me flee there—is it not a small place? Then my life will be saved.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>“Very well,” he answered, “I will grant this request as well, and will not demolish the town you indicate. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Hurry! Run there quickly, for I cannot do anything until you reach it.” That is why the town was called Zoar.<span> <a href="#genesis_19_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>And by the time the sun had risen over the land, Lot had reached Zoar. 
<span> (Luke 17:20–37)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_19_23_section">
<h3> The Destruction of Sodom and Gomorrah
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD rained down sulfur and fire on Sodom and Gomorrah—from the LORD out of the heavens. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Thus He destroyed these cities and the entire plain, including all the inhabitants of the cities and everything that grew on the ground. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>But Lot’s wife looked back, and she became a pillar of salt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Early the next morning, Abraham got up and returned to the place where he had stood before the LORD. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>He looked down toward Sodom and Gomorrah and all the land of the plain, and he saw the smoke rising from the land like smoke from a furnace. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>So when God destroyed the cities of the plain, He remembered Abraham, and He brought Lot out of the catastrophe that destroyed the cities where he had lived. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_19_29_section">
<h3> Lot and His Daughters
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Lot and his two daughters left Zoar and settled in the mountains—for he was afraid to stay in Zoar—where they lived in a cave. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>One day the older daughter said to the younger, “Our father is old, and there is no man in the land to sleep with us, as is the custom over all the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>Come, let us get our father drunk with wine so we can sleep with him and preserve his line.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>So that night they got their father drunk with wine, and the firstborn went in and slept with her father; he was not aware when she lay down or when she got up. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>The next day the older daughter said to the younger, “Look, I slept with my father last night. Let us get him drunk with wine again tonight so you can go in and sleep with him and we can preserve our father’s line.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>So again that night they got their father drunk with wine, and the younger daughter went in and slept with him; he was not aware when she lay down or when she got up. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>Thus both of Lot’s daughters became pregnant by their father. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>The older daughter gave birth to a son and named him Moab.<span> <a href="#genesis_19_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
He is the father of the Moabites of today. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_19_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>The younger daughter also gave birth to a son, and she named him Ben-ammi.<span> <a href="#genesis_19_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
He is the father of the Ammonites of today. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_19_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_19_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>19:22</em>: Zoar means small. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_19_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>19:37</em>: Moab sounds like the Hebrew for from my father. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_19_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>19:38</em>: Ben-ammi means son of my people. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_20"> 20 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_20_front">
<h3> Abraham, Sarah, and Abimelech
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Abraham journeyed from there to the region of the Negev and settled between Kadesh and Shur. While he was staying in Gerar, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Abraham said of his wife Sarah, “She is my sister.” So Abimelech king of Gerar had Sarah brought to him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>One night, however, God came to Abimelech in a dream and told him, “You are as good as dead because of the woman you have taken, for she is a married woman.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Now Abimelech had not gone near her, so he replied, “Lord, would You destroy a nation even though it is innocent? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Didn’t Abraham tell me, ‘She is my sister’? And she herself said, ‘He is my brother.’ I have done this in the integrity of my heart and the innocence of my hands.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Then God said to Abimelech in the dream, “Yes, I know that you did this with a clear conscience, and so I have kept you from sinning against Me. That is why I did not let you touch her. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Now return the man’s wife, for he is a prophet; he will pray for you and you will live. But if you do not restore her, be aware that you will surely die—you and all who belong to you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Early the next morning Abimelech got up and summoned all his servants; and when he described to them all that had happened, the men were terrified. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Then Abimelech called Abraham and asked, “What have you done to us? How have I sinned against you, that you have brought such tremendous guilt upon me and my kingdom? You have done things to me that should not be done.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Abimelech also asked Abraham, “What prompted you to do such a thing?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Abraham replied, “I thought to myself, ‘Surely there is no fear of God in this place. They will kill me on account of my wife.’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Besides, she really is my sister, the daughter of my father—though not the daughter of my mother—and she became my wife. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>So when God had me journey from my father’s house, I said to Sarah, ‘This is how you can show your loyalty to me: Wherever we go, say of me, “He is my brother.”’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So Abimelech brought sheep and cattle, menservants and maidservants, and he gave them to Abraham and restored his wife Sarah to him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And Abimelech said, “Look, my land is before you. Settle wherever you please.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>And he said to Sarah, “See, I am giving your brother a thousand pieces of silver.<span> <a href="#genesis_20_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
It is your vindication before all who are with you; you are completely cleared.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham prayed to God, and God healed Abimelech and his wife and his maidservants, so that they could again bear children—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_20_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>for on account of Abraham’s wife Sarah, the LORD had completely closed all the wombs in Abimelech’s household. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_20_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_20_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>20:16</em>: Or a thousand shekels of silver; that is, approximately 25.1 pounds or 11.4 kilograms of silver </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_21"> 21 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_21_front">
<h3> The Birth of Isaac
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now the LORD attended to Sarah as He had said, and the LORD did for Sarah what He had promised. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>So Sarah conceived and bore a son to Abraham in his old age, at the very time God had promised. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>And Abraham gave the name Isaac <span> <a href="#genesis_21_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
to the son Sarah bore to him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>When his son Isaac was eight days old, Abraham circumcised him, as God had commanded him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Abraham was a hundred years old when his son Isaac was born to him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Then Sarah said, “God has made me laugh, and everyone who hears of this will laugh with me.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>She added, “Who would have told Abraham that Sarah would nurse children? Yet I have borne him a son in his old age.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>So the child grew and was weaned, and Abraham held a great feast on the day Isaac was weaned. 
<span> (Galatians 4:21–30)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_21_8_section">
<h3> Sarah Turns against Hagar
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>But Sarah saw that the son whom Hagar the Egyptian had borne to Abraham was mocking her son,<span> <a href="#genesis_21_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>and she said to Abraham, “Expel the slave woman and her son, for the slave woman’s son will never share in the inheritance with my son Isaac!”<span> <a href="#genesis_21_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Now this matter distressed Abraham greatly because it concerned his son Ishmael. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>But God said to Abraham, “Do not be distressed about the boy and your maidservant. Listen to everything that Sarah tells you, for through Isaac your offspring will be reckoned.<span> <a href="#genesis_21_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>But I will also make a nation of the slave woman’s son, because he is your offspring.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Early in the morning, Abraham got up, took bread and a skin of water, put them on Hagar’s shoulders, and sent her away with the boy. She left and wandered in the Wilderness of Beersheba. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>When the water in the skin was gone, she left the boy under one of the bushes. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Then she went off and sat down nearby, about a bowshot away, for she said, “I cannot bear to watch the boy die!” And as she sat nearby, she lifted up her voice and wept.<span> <a href="#genesis_21_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_17"> 17 </a>
