<div>Then God heard the voice of the boy, and the angel of God called to Hagar from heaven, “What is wrong, Hagar? Do not be afraid, for God has heard the voice of the boy where he lies. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Get up, lift up the boy, and take him by the hand, for I will make him into a great nation.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Then God opened her eyes, and she saw a well of water. So she went and filled the skin with water and gave the boy a drink. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>And God was with the boy, and he grew up and settled in the wilderness and became a great archer. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>And while he was dwelling in the Wilderness of Paran, his mother got a wife for him from the land of Egypt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_21_21_section">
<h3> The Covenant at Beersheba
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>At that time Abimelech and Phicol the commander of his army said to Abraham, “God is with you in all that you do. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Now, therefore, swear to me here before God that you will not deal falsely with me or my children or descendants. Show to me and to the country in which you reside the same kindness that I have shown to you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>And Abraham replied, “I swear it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>But when Abraham complained to Abimelech about a well that Abimelech’s servants had seized, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Abimelech replied, “I do not know who has done this. You did not tell me, so I have not heard about it until today.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>So Abraham brought sheep and cattle and gave them to Abimelech, and the two men made a covenant. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>Abraham separated seven ewe lambs from the flock, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>and Abimelech asked him, “Why have you set apart these seven ewe lambs?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>He replied, “You are to accept the seven ewe lambs from my hand as my witness that I dug this well.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>So that place was called Beersheba,<span> <a href="#genesis_21_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
because it was there that the two of them swore an oath. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>After they had made the covenant at Beersheba, Abimelech and Phicol the commander of his army got up and returned to the land of the Philistines. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>And Abraham planted a tamarisk tree in Beersheba, and there he called upon the name of the LORD, the Eternal God.<span> <a href="#genesis_21_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_21_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>And Abraham resided in the land of the Philistines for a long time. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_21_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_21_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>21:3</em>: Isaac means he laughs. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_21_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>21:9</em>: LXX and Vulgate; Hebrew lacks her son </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_21_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>21:10</em>: Cited in Galatians 4:30 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_21_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>21:12</em>: Cited in Romans 9:7 and Hebrews 11:18 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_21_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>21:16</em>: Hebrew; LXX the child lifted up his voice and wept </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_21_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>21:31</em>: Beersheba means well of seven or well of the oath. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_21_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>21:33</em>: Hebrew El-Olam </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_22"> 22 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_22_front">
<h3> The Offering of Isaac
 </h3>
<span> (John 3:1–21)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Some time later God tested Abraham and said to him, “Abraham!” 
“Here I am,” he answered. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>“Take your son,” God said, “your only son Isaac, whom you love, and go to the land of Moriah. Offer him there as a burnt offering on one of the mountains, which I will show you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>So Abraham got up early the next morning, saddled his donkey, and took along two of his servants and his son Isaac. He split the wood for a burnt offering and set out for the place God had designated. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>On the third day Abraham looked up and saw the place in the distance. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>“Stay here with the donkey,” Abraham told his servants. “The boy and I will go over there to worship, and then we will return to you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Abraham took the wood for the burnt offering and placed it on his son Isaac. He himself carried the fire and the sacrificial knife, and the two of them walked on together. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Then Isaac said to his father Abraham, “My father!” 
“Here I am, my son,” he replied. 
“The fire and the wood are here,” said Isaac, “but where is the lamb for the burnt offering?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Abraham answered, “God Himself will provide the lamb for the burnt offering, my son.” And the two walked on together. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>When they arrived at the place God had designated, Abraham built the altar there and arranged the wood. He bound his son Isaac and placed him on the altar, atop the wood. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham reached out his hand and took the knife to slaughter his son. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_22_10_section">
<h3> The LORD Provides the Sacrifice
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Just then the angel <span> <a href="#genesis_22_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
of the LORD called out to him from heaven, “Abraham, Abraham!” 
“Here I am,” he replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>“Do not lay a hand on the boy or do anything to him,” said the angel, “for now I know that you fear God, since you have not withheld your only son from me.<span> <a href="#genesis_22_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham looked up and saw behind him a ram <span> <a href="#genesis_22_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
in a thicket, caught by its horns. So he went and took the ram and offered it as a burnt offering in place of his son. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>And Abraham called that place The LORD Will Provide.<span> <a href="#genesis_22_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
So to this day it is said, “On the mountain of the LORD it will be provided.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And the angel of the LORD called to Abraham from heaven a second time, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>saying, “By Myself I have sworn, declares the LORD, that because you have done this and have not withheld your only son, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>I will surely bless you, and I will multiply your descendants <span> <a href="#genesis_22_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
like the stars in the sky and the sand on the seashore. Your descendants will possess the gates of their enemies. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>And through your offspring all nations of the earth will be blessed,<span> <a href="#genesis_22_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
because you have obeyed My voice.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Abraham went back to his servants, and they got up and set out together for Beersheba. And Abraham settled in Beersheba. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_22_19_section">
<h3> The Sons of Nahor
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Some time later, Abraham was told, “Milcah has also borne sons to your brother Nahor: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Uz the firstborn, his brother Buz, Kemuel (the father of Aram), 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Chesed, Hazo, Pildash, Jidlaph, and Bethuel.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>And Bethuel became the father of Rebekah. Milcah bore these eight sons to Abraham’s brother Nahor. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_22_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Moreover, Nahor’s concubine, whose name was Reumah, bore Tebah, Gaham, Tahash, and Maacah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_22_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_22_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>22:11</em>: Or Angel; also in verses 12 and 15 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_22_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>22:12</em>: Or from Me </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_22_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>22:13</em>: Most MT manuscripts; other Hebrew manuscripts, SP, LXX, and Syriac saw one ram </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_22_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>22:14</em>: Hebrew YHWH Yireh </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_22_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>22:17</em>: Cited in Hebrews 6:14 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_22_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>22:18</em>: Cited in Acts 3:25 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_23"> 23 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_23_front">
<h3> The Death and Burial of Sarah
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Sarah lived to be 127 years old. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>She died in Kiriath-arba (that is, Hebron) in the land of Canaan, and Abraham went out to mourn and to weep for her. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham got up from beside his dead wife and said to the Hittites,<span> <a href="#genesis_23_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>“I am a foreigner and an outsider among you. Give me a burial site among you so that I can bury my dead.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>The Hittites replied to Abraham, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>“Listen to us, sir. You are God’s chosen one among us. Bury your dead in the finest of our tombs. None of us will withhold his tomb for burying your dead.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Then Abraham rose and bowed down before the people of the land, the Hittites. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>“If you are willing for me to bury my dead,” he said to them, “listen to me, and approach Ephron son of Zohar on my behalf 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>to sell me the cave of Machpelah that belongs to him; it is at the end of his field. Let him sell it to me in your presence for full price, so that I may have a burial site.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Now Ephron was sitting among the sons of Heth. So in the presence of all the Hittites who had come to the gate of his city, Ephron the Hittite answered Abraham, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>“No, my lord. Listen to me. I give you the field, and I give you the cave that is in it. I give it to you in the presence of my people. Bury your dead.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Again Abraham bowed down before the people of the land 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>and said to Ephron in their presence, “If you will please listen to me, I will pay you the price of the field. Accept it from me, so that I may bury my dead there.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Ephron answered Abraham, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>“Listen to me, my lord. The land is worth four hundred shekels of silver,<span> <a href="#genesis_23_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
but what is that between you and me? Bury your dead.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Abraham agreed to Ephron’s terms and weighed out for him the price he had named in the hearing of the Hittites: four hundred shekels of silver, according to the standard of the merchants. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>So Ephron’s field at Machpelah near Mamre, the cave that was in it, and all the trees within the boundaries of the field were deeded over 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>to Abraham’s possession in the presence of all the Hittites who had come to the gate of his city. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>After this, Abraham buried his wife Sarah in the cave of the field at Machpelah near Mamre (that is, Hebron) in the land of Canaan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_23_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>So the field and its cave were deeded by the Hittites to Abraham as a burial site. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_23_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_23_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>23:3</em>: Or sons of Heth; also in verses 5, 7, 10, 16, 18, and 20 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_23_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>23:15</em>: 400 shekels is approximately 10.1 pounds or 4.6 kilograms of silver; also in verse 16. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_24"> 24 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_24_front">
<h3> A Wife for Isaac
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>By now Abraham was old and well along in years, and the LORD had blessed him in every way. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>So Abraham instructed the chief servant of his household, who managed all he owned, “Place your hand under my thigh, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>and I will have you swear by the LORD, the God of heaven and the God of earth, that you will not take a wife for my son from the daughters of the Canaanites among whom I am dwelling, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>but will go to my country and my kindred to take a wife for my son Isaac.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>The servant asked him, “What if the woman is unwilling to follow me to this land? Shall I then take your son back to the land from which you came?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Abraham replied, “Make sure that you do not take my son back there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>The LORD, the God of heaven, who brought me from my father’s house and my native land, who spoke to me and promised me on oath, saying, ‘To your offspring I will give this land’—He will send His angel before you so that you can take a wife for my son from there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>And if the woman is unwilling to follow you, then you are released from this oath of mine. Only do not take my son back there.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>So the servant placed his hand under the thigh of his master Abraham and swore an oath to him concerning this matter. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Then the servant took ten of his master’s camels and departed with all manner of good things from his master in hand. And he set out for Nahor’s hometown in Aram-naharaim.<span> <a href="#genesis_24_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>As evening approached, he made the camels kneel down near the well outside the town at the time when the women went out to draw water. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>“O LORD, God of my master Abraham,” he prayed, “please grant me success today, and show kindness to my master Abraham. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Here I am, standing beside the spring, and the daughters of the townspeople are coming out to draw water. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Now may it happen that the girl to whom I say, ‘Please let down your jar that I may drink,’ and who responds, ‘Drink, and I will water your camels as well’—let her be the one You have appointed for Your servant Isaac. By this I will know that You have shown kindness to my master.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_24_14_section">
<h3> Rebekah Is Chosen
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Before the servant had finished praying, Rebekah came out with her jar on her shoulder. She was the daughter of Bethuel son of Milcah, the wife of Abraham’s brother Nahor. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Now the girl was very beautiful, a virgin who had not had relations with any man. She went down to the spring, filled her jar, and came up again. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>So the servant ran to meet her and said, “Please let me have a little water from your jar.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>“Drink, my lord,” she replied, and she quickly lowered her jar to her hands and gave him a drink. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>After she had given him a drink, she said, “I will also draw water for your camels, until they have had enough to drink.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>And she quickly emptied her jar into the trough and ran back to the well to draw water, until she had drawn water for all his camels. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Meanwhile, the man watched her silently to see whether or not the LORD had made his journey a success. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And after the camels had finished drinking, he took out a gold ring weighing a beka,<span> <a href="#genesis_24_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
 and two gold bracelets for her wrists weighing ten shekels.<span> <a href="#genesis_24_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>“Whose daughter are you?” he asked. “Please tell me, is there room in your father’s house for us to spend the night?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>She replied, “I am the daughter of Bethuel, the son that Milcah bore to Nahor.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Then she added, “We have plenty of straw and feed, as well as a place for you to spend the night.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Then the man bowed down and worshiped the LORD, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>saying, “Blessed be the LORD, the God of my master Abraham, who has not withheld His kindness and faithfulness from my master. As for me, the LORD has led me on the journey to the house of my master’s relatives.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>The girl ran and told her mother’s household about these things. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Now Rebekah had a brother named Laban, and he rushed out to the man at the spring. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>As soon as he saw the ring, and the bracelets on his sister’s wrists, and heard Rebekah’s words, “The man said this to me,” he went and found the man standing by the camels near the spring. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>“Come, you who are blessed by the LORD,” said Laban. “Why are you standing out here? I have prepared the house and a place for the camels.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>So the man came to the house, and the camels were unloaded. Straw and feed were brought to the camels, and water to wash his feet and the feet of his companions. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>Then a meal was set before the man, but he said, “I will not eat until I have told you what I came to say.” 
So Laban said, “Please speak.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>“I am Abraham’s servant,” he replied. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>“The LORD has greatly blessed my master, and he has become rich. He has given him sheep and cattle, silver and gold, menservants and maidservants, camels and donkeys. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>My master’s wife Sarah has borne him a son in her old age, and my master has given him everything he owns. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>My master made me swear an oath and said, ‘You shall not take a wife for my son from the daughters of the Canaanites in whose land I dwell, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>but you shall go to my father’s house and to my kindred to take a wife for my son.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_39"> 39 </a> 
<div>Then I asked my master, ‘What if the woman will not come back with me?’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_40"> 40 </a> 
<div>And he told me, ‘The LORD, before whom I have walked, will send His angel with you and make your journey a success, so that you may take a wife for my son from my kindred and from my father’s house. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_41"> 41 </a> 
<div>And when you go to my kindred, if they refuse to give her to you, then you will be released from my oath.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_42"> 42 </a> 
<div>So when I came to the spring today, I prayed: O LORD, God of my master Abraham, if only You would make my journey a success! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_43"> 43 </a> 
<div>Here I am, standing beside this spring. Now if a maiden comes out to draw water and I say to her, ‘Please let me drink a little water from your jar,’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_44"> 44 </a> 
<div>and she replies, ‘Drink, and I will draw water for your camels as well,’ may she be the woman the LORD has appointed for my master’s son. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_45"> 45 </a> 
<div>And before I had finished praying in my heart, there was Rebekah coming out with her jar on her shoulder, and she went down to the spring and drew water. So I said to her, ‘Please give me a drink.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_46"> 46 </a> 
<div>She quickly lowered her jar from her shoulder and said, ‘Drink, and I will water your camels as well.’ So I drank, and she also watered the camels. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_47"> 47 </a> 
<div>Then I asked her, ‘Whose daughter are you?’ 
She replied, ‘The daughter of Bethuel son of Nahor, whom Milcah bore to him.’ So I put the ring on her nose and the bracelets on her wrists. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_48"> 48 </a> 
<div>Then I bowed down and worshiped the LORD; and I blessed the LORD, the God of my master Abraham, who led me on the right road to take the granddaughter of my master’s brother for his son. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_49"> 49 </a> 
<div>Now if you will show kindness and faithfulness to my master, tell me; but if not, let me know, so that I may go elsewhere.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_50"> 50 </a> 
<div>Laban and Bethuel answered, “This is from the LORD; we have no choice in the matter. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_51"> 51 </a> 
<div>Rebekah is here before you. Take her and go, and let her become the wife of your master’s son, just as the LORD has decreed.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_52"> 52 </a> 
<div>When Abraham’s servant heard their words, he bowed down to the ground before the LORD. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_53"> 53 </a> 
<div>Then he brought out jewels of silver and gold, and articles of clothing, and he gave them to Rebekah. He also gave precious gifts to her brother and her mother. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_54"> 54 </a> 
<div>Then he and the men with him ate and drank and spent the night there. 
When they got up the next morning, he said, “Send me on my way to my master.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_55"> 55 </a> 
<div>But her brother and mother said, “Let the girl remain with us ten days or so. After that, she may go.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_56"> 56 </a> 
<div>But he replied, “Do not delay me, since the LORD has made my journey a success. Send me on my way so that I may go to my master.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_57"> 57 </a> 
<div>So they said, “We will call the girl and ask her opinion.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_58"> 58 </a> 
<div>They called Rebekah and asked her, “Will you go with this man?” 
“I will go,” she replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_59"> 59 </a> 
<div>So they sent their sister Rebekah on her way, along with her nurse and Abraham’s servant and his men. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_60"> 60 </a> 
<div>And they blessed Rebekah and said to her, 
“Our sister, may you become the mother 
of thousands upon thousands. 
May your offspring possess 
the gates of their enemies.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_61"> 61 </a> 
<div>Then Rebekah and her servant girls got ready, mounted the camels, and followed the man. So the servant took Rebekah and left. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_24_61_section">
<h3> Isaac Marries Rebekah
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_62"> 62 </a> 
<div>Now Isaac had just returned from Beer-lahai-roi, for he was living in the Negev. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_63"> 63 </a> 
<div>Early in the evening, Isaac went out to the field to meditate, and looking up, he saw the camels approaching. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_64"> 64 </a> 
<div>And when Rebekah looked up and saw Isaac, she got down from her camel 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_65"> 65 </a> 
<div>and asked the servant, “Who is that man in the field coming to meet us?” 
“It is my master,” the servant answered. So she took her veil and covered herself. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_66"> 66 </a> 
<div>Then the servant told Isaac all that he had done. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_24_67"> 67 </a> 
<div>And Isaac brought her into the tent of his mother Sarah and took Rebekah as his wife. And Isaac loved her and was comforted after his mother’s death. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_24_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_24_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>24:10</em>: That is, Mesopotamia; Aram-naharaim means Aram of the two rivers, likely the region between the Euphrates and Balih Rivers in northwestern Mesopotamia. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_24_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>24:22</em>: A beka is half a shekel, or approximately 0.2 ounces or 5.7 grams. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_24_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>24:22</em>: 10 shekels is approximately 4 ounces or 114 grams. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_25"> 25 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_25_front">
<h3> Abraham and Keturah
 </h3>
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:32–33)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Abraham had taken another wife, named Keturah, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>and she bore him Zimran, Jokshan, Medan, Midian, Ishbak, and Shuah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Jokshan was the father of Sheba and Dedan. And the sons of Dedan were the Asshurites, the Letushites, and the Leummites. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>The sons of Midian were Ephah, Epher, Hanoch, Abida, and Eldaah. 
All these were descendants of Keturah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Abraham left everything he owned to Isaac. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>But while he was still alive, Abraham gave gifts to the sons of his concubines and sent them away from his son Isaac to the land of the east. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_25_6_section">
<h3> The Death of Abraham
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Abraham lived a total of 175 years. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>And at a ripe old age he breathed his last and died, old and contented, and was gathered to his people. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>His sons Isaac and Ishmael buried him in the cave of Machpelah near Mamre, in the field of Ephron son of Zohar the Hittite. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>This was the field that Abraham had bought from the Hittites. Abraham was buried there with his wife Sarah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>After Abraham’s death, God blessed his son Isaac, who lived near Beer-lahai-roi. 
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:28–31)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_25_11_section">
<h3> The Descendants of Ishmael
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Abraham’s son Ishmael, whom Hagar the Egyptian, Sarah’s maidservant, bore to Abraham. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>These are the names of the sons of Ishmael in the order of their birth: Nebaioth the firstborn of Ishmael, then Kedar, Adbeel, Mibsam, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Mishma, Dumah, Massa, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Hadad, Tema, Jetur, Naphish, and Kedemah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>These were the sons of Ishmael, and these were their names by their villages and encampments—twelve princes of their tribes. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Ishmael lived a total of 137 years. Then he breathed his last and died, and was gathered to his people. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Ishmael’s descendants settled from Havilah to Shur, which is near the border of Egypt as you go toward Asshur.<span> <a href="#genesis_25_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
And they lived in hostility toward all their brothers. 
<span> (Malachi 1:1–5; Romans 9:6–29)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_25_18_section">
<h3> Jacob and Esau
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Abraham’s son Isaac. Abraham became the father of Isaac, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>and Isaac was forty years old when he married Rebekah, the daughter of Bethuel the Aramean from Paddan-aram <span> <a href="#genesis_25_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
and the sister of Laban the Aramean. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Later, Isaac prayed to the LORD on behalf of his wife, because she was barren. And the LORD heard his prayer, and his wife Rebekah conceived. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>But the children inside her struggled with each other, and she said, “Why is this happening to me?” So Rebekah went to inquire of the LORD, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>and He declared to her: 
“Two nations are in your womb, 
and two peoples from within you will be separated; 
one people will be stronger than the other, 
and the older will serve the younger.”<span> <a href="#genesis_25_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>When her time came to give birth, there were indeed twins in her womb. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>The first one came out red, covered with hair like a fur coat; so they named him Esau.<span> <a href="#genesis_25_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>After this, his brother came out grasping Esau’s heel; so he was named Jacob.<span> <a href="#genesis_25_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
And Isaac was sixty years old when the twins were born. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>When the boys grew up, Esau became a skillful hunter, a man of the field, while Jacob was a quiet man who stayed at home. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>Because Isaac had a taste for wild game, he loved Esau; but Rebekah loved Jacob. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_25_28_section">
<h3> Esau Sells His Birthright
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>One day, while Jacob was cooking some stew, Esau came in from the field and was famished. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>He said to Jacob, “Let me eat some of that red stew, for I am famished.” (That is why he was also called Edom.<span> <a href="#genesis_25_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
) 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>“First sell me your birthright,” Jacob replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>“Look,” said Esau, “I am about to die, so what good is a birthright to me?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>“Swear to me first,” Jacob said. 
So Esau swore to Jacob and sold him the birthright. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_25_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob gave some bread and lentil stew to Esau, who ate and drank and then got up and went away. Thus Esau despised his birthright. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_25_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_25_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>25:18</em>: Or Assyria </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_25_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>25:20</em>: That is, northwest Mesopotamia </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_25_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>25:23</em>: Cited in Romans 9:12 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_25_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>25:25</em>: Esau sounds like a Hebrew term that means hairy. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_25_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>25:26</em>: Jacob means he grasps the heel or he deceives. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_25_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>25:30</em>: Edom means red. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_26"> 26 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_26_front">
<h3> God’s Promise to Isaac
 </h3>
<span> (Genesis 12:1–9)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now there was another famine in the land, subsequent to the one that had occurred in Abraham’s time. And Isaac went to Abimelech king of the Philistines at Gerar. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>The LORD appeared to Isaac and said, “Do not go down to Egypt. Settle in the land where I tell you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Stay in this land as a foreigner, and I will be with you and bless you. For I will give all these lands to you and your offspring, and I will confirm the oath that I swore to your father Abraham. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>I will make your descendants as numerous as the stars in the sky, and I will give them all these lands, and through your offspring all nations of the earth will be blessed, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>because Abraham listened to My voice and kept My charge, My commandments, My statutes, and My laws.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_26_5_section">
<h3> Isaac Deceives Abimelech
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>So Isaac settled in Gerar. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>But when the men of that place asked about his wife, he said, “She is my sister.” For he was afraid to say, “She is my wife,” since he thought to himself, “The men of this place will kill me on account of Rebekah, because she is so beautiful.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>When Isaac had been there a long time, Abimelech king of the Philistines looked down from the window and was surprised to see Isaac caressing his wife Rebekah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Abimelech sent for Isaac and said, “So she is really your wife! How could you say, ‘She is my sister’?” 
Isaac replied, “Because I thought I might die on account of her.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>“What is this you have done to us?” asked Abimelech. “One of the people could easily have slept with your wife, and you would have brought guilt upon us.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>So Abimelech warned all the people, saying, “Whoever harms this man or his wife will surely be put to death.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_26_11_section">
<h3> Isaac’s Prosperity
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Now Isaac sowed seed in the land, and that very year he reaped a hundredfold. And the LORD blessed him, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>and he became richer and richer, until he was exceedingly wealthy. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>He owned so many flocks and herds and servants that the Philistines envied him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>So the Philistines took dirt and stopped up all the wells that his father’s servants had dug in the days of his father Abraham. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Then Abimelech said to Isaac, “Depart from us, for you are much too powerful for us.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>So Isaac left that place and encamped in the Valley of Gerar and settled there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Isaac reopened the wells that had been dug in the days of his father Abraham, which the Philistines had stopped up after Abraham died. And he gave these wells the same names his father had given them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Then Isaac’s servants dug in the valley and found a well of fresh water <span> <a href="#genesis_26_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>But the herdsmen of Gerar quarreled with Isaac’s herdsmen and said, “The water is ours!” So he named the well Esek,<span> <a href="#genesis_26_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
because they contended with him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Then they dug another well and quarreled over that one also; so he named it Sitnah.<span> <a href="#genesis_26_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>He moved on from there and dug another well, and they did not quarrel over it. He named it Rehoboth <span> <a href="#genesis_26_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
and said, “At last the LORD has made room for us, and we will be fruitful in the land.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>From there Isaac went up to Beersheba, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>and that night the LORD appeared to him and said, “I am the God of your father Abraham. Do not be afraid, for I am with you. I will bless you and multiply your descendants for the sake of My servant Abraham.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>So Isaac built an altar there and called on the name of the LORD, and he pitched his tent there. His servants also dug a well there. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_26_25_section">
<h3> Isaac’s Covenant with Abimelech
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Later, Abimelech came to Isaac from Gerar, with Ahuzzath his adviser and Phicol the commander of his army. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>“Why have you come to me?” Isaac asked them. “You hated me and sent me away.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>“We can plainly see that the LORD has been with you,” they replied. “We recommend that there should now be an oath between us and you. Let us make a covenant with you 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>that you will not harm us, just as we have not harmed you but have done only good to you, sending you on your way in peace. And now you are blessed by the LORD.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>So Isaac prepared a feast for them, and they ate and drank. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>And they got up early the next morning and swore an oath to each other. Then Isaac sent them on their way, and they left him in peace. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>On that same day, Isaac’s servants came and told him about the well they had dug. “We have found water!” they told him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>So he called it Shibah,<span> <a href="#genesis_26_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
 and to this day the name of the city is Beersheba.<span> <a href="#genesis_26_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_26_33_section">
<h3> Esau’s Wives
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>When Esau was forty years old, he took as his wives Judith daughter of Beeri the Hittite and Basemath daughter of Elon the Hittite. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_26_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>And they brought grief to Isaac and Rebekah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_26_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_26_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>26:19</em>: Or flowing water or living water </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_26_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>26:20</em>: Esek means contention. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_26_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>26:21</em>: Sitnah means enmity or hostility. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_26_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>26:22</em>: Rehoboth means broad places or open spaces. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_26_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>26:33</em>: Shibah can mean oath or seven. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_26_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>26:33</em>: Beersheba means well of seven or well of the oath. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_27"> 27 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_27_front">
<h3> Isaac Blesses Jacob
 </h3>
<span> (Hebrews 11:20)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>When Isaac was old and his eyes were so weak that he could no longer see, he called his older son Esau and said to him, “My son.” 
“Here I am,” Esau replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>“Look,” said Isaac, “I am now old, and I do not know the day of my death. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Take your weapons—your quiver and bow—and go out into the field to hunt some game for me. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Then prepare a tasty dish that I love and bring it to me to eat, so that I may bless you before I die.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Now Rebekah was listening to what Isaac told his son Esau. So when Esau went into the field to hunt game and bring it back, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Rebekah said to her son Jacob, “Behold, I overheard your father saying to your brother Esau, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>‘Bring me some game and prepare me a tasty dish to eat, so that I may bless you in the presence of the LORD before I die.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Now, my son, listen to my voice and do exactly as I tell you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Go out to the flock and bring me two choice young goats, so that I can make them into a tasty dish for your father—the kind he loves. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Then take it to your father to eat, so that he may bless you before he dies.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Jacob answered his mother Rebekah, “Look, my brother Esau is a hairy man, but I am smooth-skinned. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>What if my father touches me? Then I would be revealed to him as a deceiver, and I would bring upon myself a curse rather than a blessing.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>His mother replied, “Your curse be on me, my son. Just obey my voice and go get them for me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So Jacob went and got two goats and brought them to his mother, who made the tasty food his father loved. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And Rebekah took the finest clothes in the house that belonged to her older son Esau, and she put them on her younger son Jacob. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>She also put the skins of the young goats on his hands and on the smooth part of his neck. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Then she handed her son Jacob the tasty food and bread she had made. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>So Jacob went to his father and said, “My father.” 
“Here I am!” he answered. “Which one are you, my son?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Jacob said to his father, “I am Esau, your firstborn. I have done as you told me. Please sit up and eat some of my game, so that you may bless me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>But Isaac asked his son, “How did you ever find it so quickly, my son?” 
“Because the LORD your God brought it to me,” he replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Then Isaac said to Jacob, “Please come closer so I can touch you, my son. Are you really my son Esau, or not?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>So Jacob came close to his father Isaac, who touched him and said, “The voice is the voice of Jacob, but the hands are the hands of Esau.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Isaac did not recognize him, because his hands were hairy like those of his brother Esau; so he blessed him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Again he asked, “Are you really my son Esau?” 
And he replied, “I am.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>“Serve me,” said Isaac, “and let me eat some of my son’s game, so that I may bless you.” 
Jacob brought it to him, and he ate; then he brought him wine, and he drank. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Then his father Isaac said to him, “Please come near and kiss me, my son.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>So he came near and kissed him. When Isaac smelled his clothing, he blessed him and said: 
“Ah, the smell of my son 
is like the smell of a field 
that the LORD has blessed. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>May God give to you the dew of heaven 
and the richness of the earth—
an abundance of grain and new wine. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>May peoples serve you 
and nations bow down to you. 
May you be the master of your brothers, 
and may the sons of your mother bow down to you. 
May those who curse you be cursed, 
and those who bless you be blessed.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_27_29_section">
<h3> Esau’s Lost Hope
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>As soon as Isaac had finished blessing him and Jacob had left his father’s presence, his brother Esau returned from the hunt. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>He too made some tasty food, brought it to his father, and said to him, “My father, sit up and eat of your son’s game, so that you may bless me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>But his father Isaac replied, “Who are you?” 
“I am Esau, your firstborn son,” he answered. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>Isaac began to tremble violently and said, “Who was it, then, who hunted the game and brought it to me? Before you came in, I ate it all and blessed him—and indeed, he will be blessed!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>When Esau heard his father’s words, he let out a loud and bitter cry and said to his father, “Bless me too, O my father!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>But Isaac replied, “Your brother came deceitfully and took your blessing.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>So Esau declared, “Is he not rightly named Jacob?<span> <a href="#genesis_27_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
For he has cheated me twice. He took my birthright, and now he has taken my blessing.” Then he asked, “Haven’t you saved a blessing for me?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>But Isaac answered Esau: “Look, I have made him your master and given him all his relatives as servants; I have sustained him with grain and new wine. What is left that I can do for you, my son?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>Esau said to his father, “Do you have only one blessing, my father? Bless me too, O my father!” Then Esau wept aloud. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_39"> 39 </a> 
<div>His father Isaac answered him: 
“Behold, your dwelling place shall be 
away from the richness of the land, 
away from the dew of heaven above. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_40"> 40 </a> 
<div>You shall live by the sword 
and serve your brother. 
But when you rebel, 
you will tear his yoke from your neck.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_41"> 41 </a> 
<div>Esau held a grudge against Jacob because of the blessing his father had given him. And Esau said in his heart, “The days of mourning for my father are at hand; then I will kill my brother Jacob.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_42"> 42 </a> 
<div>When the words of her older son Esau were relayed to Rebekah, she sent for her younger son Jacob and told him, “Look, your brother Esau is consoling himself by plotting to kill you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_43"> 43 </a> 
<div>So now, my son, obey my voice and flee at once to my brother Laban in Haran. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_44"> 44 </a> 
<div>Stay with him for a while, until your brother’s fury subsides—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_45"> 45 </a> 
<div>until your brother’s rage against you wanes and he forgets what you have done to him. Then I will send for you and bring you back from there. Why should I lose both of you in one day?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_27_46"> 46 </a> 
<div>Then Rebekah said to Isaac, “I am weary of my life because of these Hittite women.<span> <a href="#genesis_27_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
If Jacob takes a Hittite wife from among them, what good is my life?” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_27_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_27_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>27:36</em>: Jacob means he grasps the heel or he deceives. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_27_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>27:46</em>: Or daughters of Heth </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_28"> 28 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_28_front">
<h3> Jacob’s Departure
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>So Isaac called for Jacob and blessed him. “Do not take a wife from the Canaanite women,” he commanded. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>“Go at once to Paddan-aram,<span> <a href="#genesis_28_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
to the house of your mother’s father Bethuel, and take a wife from among the daughters of Laban, your mother’s brother. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>May God Almighty <span> <a href="#genesis_28_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
bless you and make you fruitful and multiply you, so that you may become a company of peoples. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>And may He give the blessing of Abraham to you and your descendants, so that you may possess the land where you dwell as a foreigner, the land God gave to Abraham.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>So Isaac sent Jacob to Paddan-aram, to Laban son of Bethuel the Aramean, the brother of Rebekah, who was the mother of Jacob and Esau. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_28_5_section">
<h3> Esau Marries Mahalath
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Now Esau learned that Isaac had blessed Jacob and sent him to Paddan-aram to take a wife there, commanding him, “Do not marry a Canaanite woman,” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>and that Jacob had obeyed his father and mother and gone to Paddan-aram. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>And seeing that his father Isaac disapproved of the Canaanite women, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Esau went to Ishmael and married Mahalath, the sister of Nebaioth and daughter of Abraham’s son Ishmael, in addition to the wives he already had. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_28_9_section">
<h3> Jacob’s Ladder
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Meanwhile Jacob left Beersheba and set out for Haran. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>On reaching a certain place, he spent the night there because the sun had set. And taking one of the stones from that place, he put it under his head and lay down to sleep. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>And Jacob had a dream about a ladder that rested on the earth with its top reaching up to heaven, and God’s angels were going up and down the ladder. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>And there at the top <span> <a href="#genesis_28_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
the LORD was standing and saying, “I am the LORD, the God of your father Abraham and the God of Isaac. I will give you and your descendants the land on which you now lie. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Your descendants will be like the dust of the earth, and you will spread out to the west and east and north and south. All the families of the earth will be blessed through you and your offspring. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Look, I am with you, and I will watch over you wherever you go, and I will bring you back to this land. For I will not leave you until I have done what I have promised you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>When Jacob woke up, he said, “Surely the LORD is in this place, and I was unaware of it.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>And he was afraid and said, “How awesome is this place! This is none other than the house of God; this is the gate of heaven!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_28_17_section">
<h3> The Stone of Bethel
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Early the next morning, Jacob took the stone that he had placed under his head, and he set it up as a pillar. He poured oil on top of it, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>and he called that place Bethel,<span> <a href="#genesis_28_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
though previously the city had been named Luz. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob made a vow, saying, “If God will be with me and watch over me on this journey, and if He will provide me with food to eat and clothes to wear, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>so that I may return safely to my father’s house, then the LORD will be my God. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_28_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And this stone I have set up as a pillar will be God’s house, and of all that You give me I will surely give You a tenth.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_28_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_28_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>28:2</em>: That is, northwest Mesopotamia; also in verses 5, 6, and 7 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_28_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>28:3</em>: Hebrew El-Shaddai </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_28_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>28:13</em>: Or there beside him </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_28_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>28:19</em>: Bethel means house of God. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_29"> 29 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_29_front">
<h3> Jacob Meets Rachel
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Jacob resumed his journey and came to the land of the people of the east. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>He looked and saw a well in the field, and near it lay three flocks of sheep, because the sheep were watered from this well. And a large stone covered the mouth of the well. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>When all the flocks had been gathered there, the shepherds would roll away the stone from the mouth of the well and water the sheep. Then they would return the stone to its place over the mouth of the well. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>“My brothers,” Jacob asked the shepherds, “where are you from?” 
“We are from Haran,” they answered. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>“Do you know Laban the grandson of Nahor?” Jacob asked. 
“We know him,” they replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>“Is he well?” Jacob inquired. 
“Yes,” they answered, “and here comes his daughter Rachel with his sheep.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>“Look,” said Jacob, “it is still broad daylight; it is not yet time to gather the livestock. Water the sheep and take them back to pasture.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>But they replied, “We cannot, until all the flocks have been gathered and the stone has been rolled away from the mouth of the well. Then we will water the sheep.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>While he was still speaking with them, Rachel arrived with her father’s sheep, for she was a shepherdess. 
</div></div>
