<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>As soon as Jacob saw Rachel, the daughter of his mother’s brother Laban, with Laban’s sheep, he went up and rolled the stone away from the mouth of the well and watered his uncle’s sheep. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob kissed Rachel and wept aloud. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>He told Rachel that he was Rebekah’s son, a relative of her father, and she ran and told her father. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>When Laban heard the news about his sister’s son Jacob, he ran out to meet him. He embraced him and kissed him and brought him to his home, where Jacob told him all that had happened. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_29_13_section">
<h3> Jacob Marries Leah and Rachel
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Then Laban declared, “You are indeed my own flesh and blood.” 
After Jacob had stayed with him a month, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Laban said to him, “Just because you are my relative, should you work for nothing? Tell me what your wages should be.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Now Laban had two daughters; the older was named Leah, and the younger was named Rachel. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Leah had weak eyes,<span> <a href="#genesis_29_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
but Rachel was shapely and beautiful. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Since Jacob loved Rachel, he answered, “I will serve you seven years for your younger daughter Rachel.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Laban replied, “Better that I give her to you than to another. Stay here with me.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>So Jacob served seven years for Rachel, yet it seemed but a few days because of his love for her. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Finally Jacob said to Laban, “Grant me my wife, for my time is complete, and I want to sleep with her.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>So Laban invited all the men of that place and prepared a feast. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>But when evening came, Laban took his daughter Leah and gave her to Jacob, and he slept with her. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>And Laban gave his servant girl Zilpah to his daughter Leah as her maidservant. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>When morning came, there was Leah! “What have you done to me?” Jacob said to Laban. “Wasn’t it for Rachel that I served you? Why have you deceived me?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Laban replied, “It is not our custom here to give the younger daughter in marriage before the older. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Finish this week’s celebration, and we will give you the younger one in return for another seven years of work.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>And Jacob did just that. He finished the week’s celebration, and Laban gave him his daughter Rachel as his wife. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Laban also gave his servant girl Bilhah to his daughter Rachel as her maidservant. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Jacob slept with Rachel as well, and indeed, he loved Rachel more than Leah. So he worked for Laban another seven years. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_29_30_section">
<h3> Reuben, Simeon, Levi, and Judah
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>When the LORD saw that Leah was unloved, He opened her womb; but Rachel was barren. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>And Leah conceived and gave birth to a son, and she named him Reuben,<span> <a href="#genesis_29_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
for she said, “The LORD has seen my affliction. Surely my husband will love me now.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>Again she conceived and gave birth to a son, and she said, “Because the LORD has heard that I am unloved, He has given me this son as well.” So she named him Simeon.<span> <a href="#genesis_29_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>Once again Leah conceived and gave birth to a son, and she said, “Now at last my husband will become attached to me, because I have borne him three sons.” So he was named Levi.<span> <a href="#genesis_29_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_29_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>And once more she conceived and gave birth to a son and said, “This time I will praise the LORD.” So she named him Judah.<span> <a href="#genesis_29_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
Then Leah stopped having children. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_29_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_29_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>29:17</em>: Or had delicate eyes </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_29_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>29:32</em>: Reuben means Look, a son and also sounds like the Hebrew for He has seen my misery. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_29_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>29:33</em>: Simeon probably means one who hears. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_29_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>29:34</em>: Levi sounds like the Hebrew for being attached to or feeling affection for. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_29_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>29:35</em>: Judah sounds like the Hebrew for praise. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_30"> 30 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_30_front">
<h3> Dan and Naphtali
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>When Rachel saw that she was not bearing any children for Jacob, she envied her sister. “Give me children, or I will die!” she said to Jacob. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Jacob became angry with Rachel and said, “Am I in the place of God, who has withheld children from you?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Then she said, “Here is my maidservant Bilhah. Sleep with her, that she may bear children for me,<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
so that through her I too can build a family.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>So Rachel gave Jacob her servant Bilhah as a wife, and he slept with her, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>and Bilhah conceived and bore him a son. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Then Rachel said, “God has vindicated me; He has heard my plea and given me a son.” So she named him Dan.<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And Rachel’s servant Bilhah conceived again and bore Jacob a second son. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then Rachel said, “In my great struggles, I have wrestled with my sister and won.” So she named him Naphtali.<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_30_8_section">
<h3> Gad and Asher
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>When Leah saw that she had stopped having children, she gave her servant Zilpah to Jacob as a wife. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>And Leah’s servant Zilpah bore Jacob a son. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Then Leah said, “How fortunate!”<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
 So she named him Gad.<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>When Leah’s servant Zilpah bore Jacob a second son, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Leah said, “How happy I am! For the women call me happy.” So she named him Asher.<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Now during the wheat harvest, Reuben went out and found some mandrakes in the field. When he brought them to his mother, Rachel begged Leah, “Please give me some of your son’s mandrakes.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>But Leah replied, “Is it not enough that you have taken away my husband? Now you want to take my son’s mandrakes as well?” 
“Very well,” said Rachel, “he may sleep with you tonight in exchange for your son’s mandrakes.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>When Jacob came in from the field that evening, Leah went out to meet him and said, “You must come with me, for I have hired you with my son’s mandrakes.” So he slept with her that night. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_30_16_section">
<h3> Issachar, Zebulun, and Dinah
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>And God listened to Leah, and she conceived and bore a fifth son to Jacob. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Then Leah said, “God has rewarded me for giving my maidservant to my husband.” So she named him Issachar.<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Again Leah conceived and bore a sixth son to Jacob. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>“God has given me a good gift,” she said. “This time my husband will honor me, because I have borne him six sons.” And she named him Zebulun.<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_8">[8]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>After that, Leah gave birth to a daughter and named her Dinah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_30_21_section">
<h3> Joseph
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Then God remembered Rachel. He listened to her and opened her womb, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>and she conceived and gave birth to a son. “God has taken away my shame,” she said. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>She named him Joseph,<span> <a href="#genesis_30_footnote_9">[9]</a> </span>
and said, “May the LORD add to me another son.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_30_24_section">
<h3> Jacob Prospers
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Now after Rachel had given birth to Joseph, Jacob said to Laban, “Send me on my way so I can return to my homeland. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Give me my wives and children for whom I have served you, that I may go on my way. You know how hard I have worked for you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>But Laban replied, “If I have found favor in your eyes, please stay. I have learned by divination that the LORD has blessed me because of you.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>And he added, “Name your wages, and I will pay them.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob answered, “You know how I have served you and how your livestock have thrived under my care. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Indeed, you had very little before my arrival, but now your wealth has increased many times over. The LORD has blessed you wherever I set foot. But now, when may I also provide for my own household?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>“What can I give you?” Laban asked. 
“You do not need to give me anything,” Jacob replied. “If you do this one thing for me, I will keep on shepherding and keeping your flocks. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>Let me go through all your flocks today and remove from them every speckled or spotted sheep, every dark-colored lamb, and every spotted or speckled goat. These will be my wages. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>So my honesty will testify for me when you come to check on my wages in the future. If I have any goats that are not speckled or spotted, or any lambs that are not dark-colored, they will be considered stolen.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>“Agreed,” said Laban. “Let it be as you have said.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>That very day Laban removed all the streaked or spotted male goats and every speckled or spotted female goat—every one that had any white on it—and every dark-colored lamb, and he placed them under the care of his sons. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>Then he put a three-day journey between himself and Jacob, while Jacob was shepherding the rest of Laban’s flocks. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>Jacob, however, took fresh branches of poplar, almond, and plane trees, and peeled the bark, exposing the white inner wood of the branches. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>Then he set the peeled branches in the watering troughs in front of the flocks coming in to drink. So when the flocks were in heat and came to drink, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_39"> 39 </a> 
<div>they mated in front of the branches. And they bore young that were streaked or speckled or spotted. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_40"> 40 </a> 
<div>Jacob set apart the young, but made the rest face the streaked dark-colored sheep in Laban’s flocks. Then he set his own stock apart and did not put them with Laban’s animals. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_41"> 41 </a> 
<div>Whenever the stronger females of the flock were in heat, Jacob would place the branches in the troughs, in full view of the animals, so that they would breed in front of the branches. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_42"> 42 </a> 
<div>But if the animals were weak, he did not set out the branches. So the weaker animals went to Laban and the stronger ones to Jacob. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_30_43"> 43 </a> 
<div>Thus Jacob became exceedingly prosperous. He owned large flocks, maidservants and menservants, and camels and donkeys. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_30_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>30:3</em>: Literally bear children on my knees </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>30:6</em>: Dan means He has judged or He has vindicated. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>30:8</em>: Naphtali sounds like the Hebrew for wrestling. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>30:11</em>: Alternate MT reading (see also LXX); the other alternate reads “A troop is coming!” </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>30:11</em>: Gad sounds like the Hebrew for good fortune, or alternately for band of raiders. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>30:13</em>: Asher means happy. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>30:18</em>: Issachar sounds like the Hebrew for wages or reward. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_8>[8]</a> <em>30:20</em>: Zebulun sounds like the Hebrew for honor. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_30_footnote_9>[9]</a> <em>30:24</em>: Joseph means may He add. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_31"> 31 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_31_front">
<h3> Jacob Flees from Laban
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Jacob heard that Laban’s sons were saying, “Jacob has taken away all that belonged to our father and built all this wealth at our father’s expense.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And Jacob saw from the countenance of Laban that his attitude toward him had changed. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Then the LORD said to Jacob, “Go back to the land of your fathers and to your kindred, and I will be with you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>So Jacob sent word and called Rachel and Leah to the field where his flocks were, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>and he told them, “I can see from your father’s countenance that his attitude toward me has changed; but the God of my father has been with me. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>You know that I have served your father with all my strength. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And although he has cheated me and changed my wages ten times, God has not allowed him to harm me. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>If he said, ‘The speckled will be your wages,’ then the whole flock bore speckled offspring. If he said, ‘The streaked will be your wages,’ then the whole flock bore streaked offspring. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Thus God has taken away your father’s livestock and given them to me. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>When the flocks were breeding, I saw in a dream that the streaked, spotted, and speckled males were mating with the females. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>In that dream the angel <span> <a href="#genesis_31_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
of God said to me, ‘Jacob!’ 
And I replied, ‘Here I am.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>‘Look up,’ he said, ‘and see that all the males that are mating with the flock are streaked, spotted, or speckled; for I have seen all that Laban has done to you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>I am the God of Bethel, where you anointed the pillar and made a solemn vow to Me. Now get up, leave this land at once, and return to your native land.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>And Rachel and Leah replied, “Do we have any portion or inheritance left in our father’s house? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Are we not regarded by him as outsiders? Not only has he sold us, but he has certainly squandered what was paid for us. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Surely all the wealth that God has taken away from our father belongs to us and to our children. So do whatever God has told you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob got up and put his children and his wives on camels, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>and he drove all his livestock before him, along with all the possessions he had acquired in Paddan-aram,<span> <a href="#genesis_31_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
to go to his father Isaac in the land in Canaan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Now while Laban was out shearing his sheep, Rachel stole her father’s household idols. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Moreover, Jacob deceived <span> <a href="#genesis_31_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
Laban the Aramean by not telling him that he was running away. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>So he fled with all his possessions, crossed the Euphrates,<span> <a href="#genesis_31_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
and headed for the hill country of Gilead. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_31_21_section">
<h3> Laban Pursues Jacob
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>On the third day Laban was informed that Jacob had fled. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>So he took his relatives with him, pursued Jacob for seven days, and overtook him in the hill country of Gilead. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>But that night God came to Laban the Aramean in a dream and warned him, “Be careful not to say anything to Jacob, either good or bad.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Now Jacob had pitched his tent in the hill country of Gilead when Laban overtook him, and Laban and his relatives camped there as well. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Then Laban said to Jacob, “What have you done? You have deceived me and carried off my daughters like captives of war! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Why did you run away secretly and deceive me, without even telling me? I would have sent you away with joy and singing, with tambourines and harps. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>But you did not even let me kiss my grandchildren and my daughters goodbye. Now you have done a foolish thing. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>I have power to do you great harm, but last night the God of your father said to me, ‘Be careful not to say anything to Jacob, either good or bad.’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Now you have gone off because you long for your father’s house. But why have you stolen my gods?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>“I was afraid,” Jacob answered, “for I thought you would take your daughters from me by force. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>If you find your gods with anyone here, he shall not live! In the presence of our relatives, see for yourself if anything is yours, and take it back.” For Jacob did not know that Rachel had stolen the idols. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>So Laban went into Jacob’s tent, then Leah’s tent, and then the tents of the two maidservants, but he found nothing. Then he left Leah’s tent and entered Rachel’s tent. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>Now Rachel had taken Laban’s household idols, put them in the saddlebag of her camel, and was sitting on them. And Laban searched everything in the tent but found nothing. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>Rachel said to her father, “Sir, do not be angry that I cannot stand up before you; for I am having my period.” So Laban searched but could not find the household idols. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob became incensed and challenged Laban. “What is my crime?” he said. “For what sin of mine have you so hotly pursued me? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>You have searched all my goods! Have you found anything that belongs to you? Put it here before my brothers and yours, that they may judge between the two of us. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>I have been with you for twenty years now. Your sheep and goats have not miscarried, nor have I eaten the rams of your flock. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_39"> 39 </a> 
<div>I did not bring you anything torn by wild beasts; I bore the loss myself. And you demanded payment from me for what was stolen by day or night. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_40"> 40 </a> 
<div>As it was, the heat consumed me by day and the frost by night, and sleep fled from my eyes. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_41"> 41 </a> 
<div>Thus for twenty years I have served in your household—fourteen years for your two daughters and six years for your flocks—and you have changed my wages ten times! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_42"> 42 </a> 
<div>If the God of my father, the God of Abraham and the Fear of Isaac, had not been with me, surely by now you would have sent me away empty-handed. But God has seen my affliction and the toil of my hands, and last night He rendered judgment.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_31_42_section">
<h3> Jacob’s Covenant with Laban
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_43"> 43 </a> 
<div>But Laban answered Jacob, “These daughters are my daughters, these sons are my sons, and these flocks are my flocks! Everything you see is mine! Yet what can I do today about these daughters of mine or the children they have borne? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_44"> 44 </a> 
<div>Come now, let us make a covenant, you and I, and let it serve as a witness between you and me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_45"> 45 </a> 
<div>So Jacob picked out a stone and set it up as a pillar, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_46"> 46 </a> 
<div>and he said to his relatives, “Gather some stones.” So they took stones and made a mound, and there by the mound they ate. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_47"> 47 </a> 
<div>Laban called it Jegar-sahadutha, and Jacob called it Galeed.<span> <a href="#genesis_31_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_48"> 48 </a> 
<div>Then Laban declared, “This mound is a witness between you and me this day.” 
Therefore the place was called Galeed. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_49"> 49 </a> 
<div>It was also called Mizpah,<span> <a href="#genesis_31_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
because Laban said, “May the LORD keep watch between you and me when we are absent from each other. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_50"> 50 </a> 
<div>If you mistreat my daughters or take other wives, although no one is with us, remember that God is a witness between you and me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_51"> 51 </a> 
<div>Laban also said to Jacob, “Here is the mound, and here is the pillar I have set up between you and me. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_52"> 52 </a> 
<div>This mound is a witness, and this pillar is a witness, that I will not go past this mound to harm you, and you will not go past this mound and pillar to harm me. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_53"> 53 </a> 
<div>May the God of Abraham and the God of Nahor, the God of their father, judge between us.” 
So Jacob swore by the Fear of his father Isaac. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_54"> 54 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob offered a sacrifice on the mountain and invited his relatives to eat a meal. And after they had eaten, they spent the night on the mountain. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_31_55"> 55 </a> 
<div>Early the next morning, Laban got up and kissed his grandchildren and daughters and blessed them. Then he left to return home. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_31_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_31_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>31:11</em>: Or Angel </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_31_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>31:18</em>: That is, northwest Mesopotamia </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_31_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>31:20</em>: Or stole the heart of; also in verses 26 and 27 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_31_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>31:21</em>: Hebrew the River </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_31_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>31:47</em>: The Aramaic Jegar-sahadutha and the Hebrew Galeed both mean heap of witnesses. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_31_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>31:49</em>: Mizpah means watchtower. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_32"> 32 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_32_front">
<h3> Jacob Prepares to Meet Esau
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Jacob also went on his way, and the angels of God met him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>When Jacob saw them, he said, “This is the camp of God.” So he named that place Mahanaim.<span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Jacob sent messengers ahead of him to his brother Esau in the land of Seir, the country of Edom. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>He instructed them, “You are to say to my master Esau, ‘Your servant Jacob says: I have been staying with Laban and have remained there until now. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>I have oxen, donkeys, flocks, menservants, and maidservants. I have sent this message to inform my master, so that I may find favor in your sight.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>When the messengers returned to Jacob, they said, “We went to your brother Esau, and now he is coming to meet you—he and four hundred men with him.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>In great fear and distress, Jacob divided his people into two camps, as well as the flocks and herds and camels. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>He thought, “If Esau comes and attacks one camp, then the other camp can escape.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob declared, “O God of my father Abraham, God of my father Isaac, the LORD who told me, ‘Go back to your country and to your kindred, and I will make you prosper,’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>I am unworthy of all the kindness and faithfulness You have shown Your servant. Indeed, with only my staff I came across the Jordan, but now I have become two camps. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Please deliver me from the hand of my brother Esau, for I am afraid that he may come and attack me and the mothers and children with me. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>But You have said, ‘I will surely make you prosper, and I will make your offspring like the sand of the sea, too numerous to count.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Jacob spent the night there, and from what he had brought with him, he selected a gift for his brother Esau: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>200 female goats, 20 male goats, 200 ewes, 20 rams, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>30 milk camels with their young, 40 cows, 10 bulls, 20 female donkeys, and 10 male donkeys. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>He entrusted them to his servants in separate herds and told them, “Go on ahead of me, and keep some distance between the herds.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>He instructed the one in the lead, “When my brother Esau meets you and asks, ‘To whom do you belong, where are you going, and whose animals are these before you?’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>then you are to say, ‘They belong to your servant Jacob. They are a gift, sent to my lord Esau. And behold, Jacob is behind us.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>He also instructed the second, the third, and all those following behind the herds: “When you meet Esau, you are to say the same thing to him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>You are also to say, ‘Look, your servant Jacob is right behind us.’” For he thought, “I will appease Esau <span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
 with the gift that is going before me. After that I can face him, and perhaps he will accept me.<span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>So Jacob’s gifts went on before him, while he spent the night in the camp. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_32_21_section">
<h3> Jacob Wrestles with God
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>During the night Jacob got up and took his two wives, his two maidservants, and his eleven sons, and crossed the ford of the Jabbok. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>He took them and sent them across the stream, along with all his possessions. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>So Jacob was left all alone, and there a man <span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
wrestled with him until daybreak. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>When the man saw that he could not overpower Jacob, he struck the socket of Jacob’s hip and dislocated it as they wrestled. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Then the man said, “Let me go, for it is daybreak.” 
But Jacob replied, “I will not let you go unless you bless me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>“What is your name?” the man asked. 
“Jacob,” he replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>Then the man said, “Your name will no longer be Jacob,<span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
 but Israel,<span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
because you have struggled with God and with men, and you have prevailed.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>And Jacob requested, “Please tell me your name.” 
But he replied, “Why do you ask my name?” Then he blessed Jacob there. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>So Jacob named the place Peniel,<span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>
saying, “Indeed, I have seen God face to face, and yet my life was spared.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>The sun rose above him as he passed by Penuel,<span> <a href="#genesis_32_footnote_8">[8]</a> </span>
and he was limping because of his hip. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_32_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>Therefore to this day the Israelites do not eat the tendon attached to the socket of the hip, because the socket of Jacob’s hip was struck near that tendon. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_32_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>32:2</em>: Mahanaim means two camps. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>32:20</em>: Or I will appease his face </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>32:20</em>: Literally perhaps he will lift up my face </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>32:24</em>: Or Man; here and in verses 25–28; corresponding pronouns may also be capitalized </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>32:28</em>: Jacob means he grasps the heel or he deceives. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>32:28</em>: Israel means he struggles with God. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>32:30</em>: Peniel means the face of God. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_32_footnote_8>[8]</a> <em>32:31</em>: Penuel is a variant of Peniel; see verse 30. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_33"> 33 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_33_front">
<h3> Jacob Meets Esau
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Jacob looked up and saw Esau coming toward him with four hundred men. So he divided the children among Leah, Rachel, and the two maidservants. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>He put the maidservants and their children in front, Leah and her children next, and Rachel and Joseph at the rear. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>But Jacob himself went on ahead and bowed to the ground seven times as he approached his brother. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Esau, however, ran to him and embraced him, threw his arms around his neck, and kissed him. And they both wept. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>When Esau looked up and saw the women and children, he asked, “Who are these with you?” 
Jacob answered, “These are the children God has graciously given your servant.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Then the maidservants and their children approached and bowed down. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Leah and her children also approached and bowed down, and then Joseph and Rachel approached and bowed down. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>“What do you mean by sending this whole company to meet me?” asked Esau. 
“To find favor in your sight, my lord,” Jacob answered. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>“I already have plenty, my brother,” Esau replied. “Keep what belongs to you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>But Jacob insisted, “No, please! If I have found favor in your sight, then receive this gift from my hand. For indeed, I have seen your face, and it is like seeing the face of God, since you have received me favorably. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Please accept my gift <span> <a href="#genesis_33_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
that was brought to you, because God has been gracious to me and I have all I need.” So Jacob pressed him until he accepted. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Then Esau said, “Let us be on our way, and I will go ahead of you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>But Jacob replied, “My lord knows that the children are frail, and I must care for sheep and cattle that are nursing their young. If they are driven hard for even a day, all the animals will die. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Please let my lord go ahead of his servant. I will continue on slowly, at a comfortable pace for the livestock and children, until I come to my lord at Seir.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>“Let me leave some of my people with you,” Esau said. 
But Jacob replied, “Why do that? Let me find favor in the sight of my lord.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>So that day Esau started on his way back to Seir, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>but Jacob went on to Succoth,<span> <a href="#genesis_33_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
where he built a house for himself and shelters for his livestock; that is why the place was called Succoth. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_33_17_section">
<h3> Jacob Settles in Shechem
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>After Jacob had come from Paddan-aram,<span> <a href="#genesis_33_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
he arrived safely at the city of Shechem in the land of Canaan, and he camped just outside the city. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>And the plot of ground where he pitched his tent, he purchased from the sons of Hamor, Shechem’s father, for a hundred pieces of silver.<span> <a href="#genesis_33_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_33_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>There he set up an altar and called it El-Elohe-Israel.<span> <a href="#genesis_33_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_33_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_33_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>33:11</em>: Or blessing or treaty of peace </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_33_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>33:17</em>: Succoth means booths or shelters or tabernacles; twice in this verse. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_33_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>33:18</em>: That is, northwest Mesopotamia </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_33_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>33:19</em>: Hebrew a hundred kesitahs; the value or weight of the kesitah is no longer known </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_33_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>33:20</em>: El-Elohe-Israel means God is the God of Israel or mighty is the God of Israel. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_34"> 34 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_34_front">
<h3> The Defiling of Dinah
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Dinah, the daughter Leah had borne to Jacob, went out to visit the daughters of the land. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>When Shechem son of Hamor the Hivite, the prince of the region, saw her, he took her and lay with her by force. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>And his soul was drawn to Dinah, the daughter of Jacob. He loved the young girl and spoke to her tenderly. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>So Shechem told his father Hamor, “Get me this girl as a wife.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Jacob heard that Shechem had defiled his daughter Dinah, but since his sons were with his livestock in the field, he remained silent about it until they returned. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Meanwhile, Shechem’s father Hamor came to speak with Jacob. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>When Jacob’s sons heard what had happened, they returned from the field. They were filled with grief and fury, because Shechem had committed an outrage in Israel <span> <a href="#genesis_34_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
by lying with Jacob’s daughter—a thing that should not be done. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>But Hamor said to them, “My son Shechem longs for your daughter. Please give her to him as his wife. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Intermarry with us; give us your daughters and take our daughters for yourselves. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>You may settle among us, and the land will be open to you. Live here, move about freely, and acquire your own property.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Then Shechem said to Dinah’s father and brothers, “Grant me this favor, and I will give you whatever you ask. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Demand a high dowry and an expensive gift, and I will give you whatever you ask. Only give me the girl as my wife!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_34_12_section">
<h3> The Revenge of Dinah’s Brothers
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>But because Shechem had defiled their sister Dinah, Jacob’s sons answered him and his father Hamor deceitfully. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>“We cannot do such a thing,” they said. “To give our sister to an uncircumcised man would be a disgrace to us. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>We will consent to this on one condition, that you become circumcised like us—every one of your males. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Then we will give you our daughters and take your daughters for ourselves. We will dwell among you and become one people. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>But if you will not agree to be circumcised, then we will take our sister and go.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Their offer seemed good to Hamor and his son Shechem. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>The young man, who was the most respected of all his father’s household, did not hesitate to fulfill this request, because he was delighted with Jacob’s daughter. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>So Hamor and his son Shechem went to the gate of their city and addressed the men of their city: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>“These men are at peace with us. Let them live and trade in our land; indeed, it is large enough for them. Let us take their daughters in marriage and give our daughters to them. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>But only on this condition will the men agree to dwell with us and be one people: if all our men are circumcised as they are. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Will not their livestock, their possessions, and all their animals become ours? Only let us consent to them, and they will dwell among us.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>All the men who went out of the city gate listened to Hamor and his son Shechem, and every male of the city was circumcised. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Three days later, while they were still in pain, two of Jacob’s sons (Dinah’s brothers Simeon and Levi) took their swords, went into the unsuspecting city, and slaughtered every male. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>They killed Hamor and his son Shechem with their swords, took Dinah out of Shechem’s house, and went away. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Jacob’s other sons came upon the slaughter and looted the city, because their sister had been defiled. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>They took their flocks and herds and donkeys, and everything else in the city or in the field. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>They carried off all their possessions and women and children, and they plundered everything in their houses. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob said to Simeon and Levi, “You have brought trouble upon me by making me a stench to the Canaanites and Perizzites, the people of this land. We are few in number; if they unite against me and attack me, I and my household will be destroyed.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_34_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>But they replied, “Should he have treated our sister like a prostitute?” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_34_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_34_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>34:7</em>: Or against Israel </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_35"> 35 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_35_front">
<h3> Jacob Returns to Bethel
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then God said to Jacob, “Arise, go up to Bethel, and settle there. Build an altar there to the God who appeared to you when you fled from your brother Esau.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>So Jacob told his household and all who were with him, “Get rid of the foreign gods that are among you. Purify yourselves and change your garments. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Then let us arise and go to Bethel. I will build an altar there to God, who answered me in my day of distress. He has been with me wherever I have gone.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>So they gave Jacob all their foreign gods and all their earrings, and Jacob buried them under the oak <span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
near Shechem. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>As they set out, a terror from God fell over the surrounding cities, so that they did not pursue Jacob’s sons. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>So Jacob and everyone with him arrived in Luz (that is, Bethel) in the land of Canaan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>There Jacob built an altar, and he called that place El-bethel,<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
because it was there that God had revealed Himself to Jacob as he fled from his brother. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Now Deborah, Rebekah’s nurse, died and was buried under the oak <span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
 below Bethel. So Jacob named it Allon-bacuth.<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>After Jacob had returned from Paddan-aram,<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
God appeared to him again and blessed him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>And God said to him, “Though your name is Jacob,<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
 you will no longer be called Jacob. Instead, your name will be Israel.<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>
” So God named him Israel. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And God told him, “I am God Almighty.<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_8">[8]</a> </span>
Be fruitful and multiply. A nation—even a company of nations—shall come from you, and kings shall descend from you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>The land that I gave to Abraham and Isaac I will give to you, and I will give this land to your descendants after you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Then God went up from the place where He had spoken with him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So Jacob set up a pillar in the place where God had spoken with him—a stone marker—and he poured out a drink offering on it and anointed it with oil. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Jacob called the place where God had spoken with him Bethel.<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_9">[9]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_35_15_section">
<h3> Benjamin Born, Rachel Dies
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Later, they set out from Bethel, and while they were still some distance from Ephrath, Rachel began to give birth, and her labor was difficult. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>During her severe labor, the midwife said to her, “Do not be afraid, for you are having another son.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>And with her last breath—for she was dying—she named him Ben-oni.<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_10">[10]</a> </span>
 But his father called him Benjamin.<span> <a href="#genesis_35_footnote_11">[11]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>So Rachel died and was buried on the way to Ephrath (that is, Bethlehem). 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Jacob set up a pillar on her grave; it marks Rachel’s tomb to this day. 
<span> (1 Chronicles 2:1–2)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_35_20_section">
<h3> The Sons of Jacob
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Israel again set out and pitched his tent beyond the Tower of Eder. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>While Israel was living in that region, Reuben went in and slept with his father’s concubine Bilhah, and Israel heard about it. 
Jacob had twelve sons: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>The sons of Leah were Reuben the firstborn of Jacob, Simeon, Levi, Judah, Issachar, and Zebulun. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>The sons of Rachel were Joseph and Benjamin. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>The sons of Rachel’s maidservant Bilhah were Dan and Naphtali. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>And the sons of Leah’s maidservant Zilpah were Gad and Asher. 
These are the sons of Jacob, who were born to him in Paddan-aram. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_35_26_section">
<h3> The Death of Isaac
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Jacob returned to his father Isaac at Mamre, near Kiriath-arba (that is, Hebron), where Abraham and Isaac had stayed. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>And Isaac lived 180 years. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_35_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Then he breathed his last and died and was gathered to his people, old and full of years. And his sons Esau and Jacob buried him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_35_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>35:4</em>: Or terebinth or great tree </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>35:7</em>: El-bethel means God of Bethel. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>35:8</em>: Or great tree </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>35:8</em>: Allon-bacuth means oak of weeping. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>35:9</em>: That is, northwest Mesopotamia; also in verse 26 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>35:10</em>: Jacob means he grasps the heel or he deceives. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>35:10</em>: Israel means he struggles with God. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_8>[8]</a> <em>35:11</em>: Hebrew El-Shaddai </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_9>[9]</a> <em>35:15</em>: Bethel means house of God. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_10>[10]</a> <em>35:18</em>: Ben-oni could mean son of my sorrow or son of my strength. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_35_footnote_11>[11]</a> <em>35:18</em>: Benjamin means son of my right hand. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_36"> 36 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_36_front">
<h3> The Descendants of Esau
 </h3>
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:35–37)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Esau (that is, Edom). 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Esau took his wives from the daughters of Canaan: Adah daughter of Elon the Hittite, Oholibamah daughter of Anah and granddaughter of Zibeon the Hivite, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>and Basemath daughter of Ishmael and sister of Nebaioth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>And Adah bore Eliphaz to Esau, Basemath gave birth to Reuel, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>and Oholibamah gave birth to Jeush, Jalam, and Korah. These were the sons of Esau, who were born to him in the land of Canaan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Later, Esau took his wives and sons and daughters and all the people of his household, along with his livestock, all his other animals, and all the property he had acquired in Canaan, and he moved to a land far away from his brother Jacob. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>For their possessions were too great for them to dwell together; the land where they stayed could not support them because of their livestock. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>So Esau (that is, Edom) settled in the area of Mount Seir. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Esau, the father of the Edomites, in the area of Mount Seir. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>These are the names of Esau’s sons: Eliphaz son of Esau’s wife Adah, and Reuel son of Esau’s wife Basemath. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>The sons of Eliphaz were Teman, Omar, Zepho, Gatam, and Kenaz. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Additionally, Timna, a concubine of Esau’s son Eliphaz, gave birth to Amalek. These are the grandsons of Esau’s wife Adah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Reuel: Nahath, Zerah, Shammah, and Mizzah. They are the grandsons of Esau’s wife Basemath. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Esau’s wife Oholibamah (daughter of Anah and granddaughter of Zibeon) whom she bore to Esau: Jeush, Jalam, and Korah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>These are the chiefs among the sons of Esau. The sons of Eliphaz the firstborn of Esau: Chiefs Teman, Omar, Zepho, Kenaz, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Korah,<span> <a href="#genesis_36_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
Gatam, and Amalek. They are the chiefs of Eliphaz in the land of Edom, and they are the grandsons of Adah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Esau’s son Reuel: Chiefs Nahath, Zerah, Shammah, and Mizzah. They are the chiefs descended from Reuel in the land of Edom, and they are the grandsons of Esau’s wife Basemath. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Esau’s wife Oholibamah: Chiefs Jeush, Jalam, and Korah. They are the chiefs descended from Esau’s wife Oholibamah, the daughter of Anah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>All these are the sons of Esau (that is, Edom), and they were their chiefs. 
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:38–42)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_36_19_section">
<h3> The Descendants of Seir
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Seir the Horite, who were living in the land: Lotan, Shobal, Zibeon, Anah, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Dishon, Ezer, and Dishan. They are the chiefs of the Horites, the descendants of Seir in the land of Edom. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>The sons of Lotan were Hori and Hemam.<span> <a href="#genesis_36_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
Timna was Lotan’s sister. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Shobal: Alvan, Manahath, Ebal, Shepho, and Onam. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Zibeon: Aiah and Anah. (This is the Anah who found the hot springs in the wilderness as he was pasturing the donkeys of his father Zibeon.) 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>These are the children of Anah: Dishon and Oholibamah daughter of Anah. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Dishon:<span> <a href="#genesis_36_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
Hemdan, Eshban, Ithran, and Cheran. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Ezer: Bilhan, Zaavan, and Akan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Dishan: Uz and Aran. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>These are the chiefs of the Horites: Chiefs Lotan, Shobal, Zibeon, Anah, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Dishon, Ezer, and Dishan. They are the chiefs of the Horites, according to their divisions in the land of Seir. 
<span> (1 Chronicles 1:43–54)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_36_30_section">
<h3> The Kings of Edom
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>These are the kings who reigned in the land of Edom before any king reigned over the Israelites:<span> <a href="#genesis_36_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>Bela son of Beor reigned in Edom; the name of his city was Dinhabah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>When Bela died, Jobab son of Zerah from Bozrah reigned in his place. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>When Jobab died, Husham from the land of the Temanites reigned in his place. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>When Husham died, Hadad son of Bedad, who defeated Midian in the country of Moab, reigned in his place. And the name of his city was Avith. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>When Hadad died, Samlah from Masrekah reigned in his place. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
