<a id="genesis_36_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>When Samlah died, Shaul from Rehoboth on the Euphrates <span> <a href="#genesis_36_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
reigned in his place. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>When Shaul died, Baal-hanan son of Achbor reigned in his place. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_39"> 39 </a> 
<div>When Baal-hanan son of Achbor died, Hadad <span> <a href="#genesis_36_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
reigned in his place. His city was named Pau, and his wife’s name was Mehetabel daughter of Matred, the daughter of Me-zahab. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_40"> 40 </a> 
<div>These are the names of Esau’s chiefs, according to their families and regions, by their names: Chiefs Timna, Alvah, Jetheth, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_41"> 41 </a> 
<div>Oholibamah, Elah, Pinon, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_42"> 42 </a> 
<div>Kenaz, Teman, Mibzar, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_36_43"> 43 </a> 
<div>Magdiel, and Iram. These were the chiefs of Edom, according to their settlements in the land they possessed. Esau was the father of the Edomites. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_36_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_36_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>36:16</em>: Hebrew; SP (also in verse 11 and 1 Chronicles 1:36) does not include Korah. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_36_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>36:22</em>: Hemam is a variant of Homam; see 1 Chronicles 1:39. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_36_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>36:26</em>: Hebrew Dishan, a variant of Dishon </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_36_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>36:31</em>: Or before an Israelite king reigned over them: </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_36_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>36:37</em>: Hebrew the River </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_36_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>36:39</em>: Some MT manuscripts, SP, and Syriac (see also 1 Chronicles 1:50); other MT manuscripts Hadar </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_37"> 37 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_37_front">
<h3> Joseph’s Dreams
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now Jacob lived in the land where his father had resided, the land of Canaan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>This is the account of Jacob. When Joseph was seventeen years old, he was tending the flock with his brothers, the sons of his father’s wives Bilhah and Zilpah, and he brought their father a bad report about them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Now Israel loved Joseph more than his other sons, because Joseph had been born to him in his old age; so he made him a robe of many colors.<span> <a href="#genesis_37_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>When Joseph’s brothers saw that their father loved him more than any of them, they hated him and could not speak a kind word to him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph had a dream, and when he told it to his brothers, they hated him even more. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>He said to them, “Listen to this dream I had: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>We were binding sheaves of grain in the field, and suddenly my sheaf rose and stood upright, while your sheaves gathered around and bowed down to mine.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>“Do you intend to reign over us?” his brothers asked. “Will you actually rule us?” So they hated him even more because of his dream and his statements. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph had another dream and told it to his brothers. “Look,” he said, “I had another dream, and this time the sun and moon and eleven stars were bowing down to me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>He told his father and brothers, but his father rebuked him and said, “What is this dream that you have had? Will your mother and brothers and I actually come and bow down to the ground before you?” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And his brothers were jealous of him, but his father kept in mind what he had said. 
<span> (Acts 7:9–14)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_37_11_section">
<h3> Joseph Sold into Egypt
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Some time later, Joseph’s brothers had gone to pasture their father’s flocks near Shechem. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Israel said to him, “Are not your brothers pasturing the flocks at Shechem? Get ready; I am sending you to them.” 
“I am ready,” Joseph replied. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Then Israel told him, “Go now and see how your brothers and the flocks are faring, and bring word back to me.” 
So he sent him off from the Valley of Hebron. And when Joseph arrived in Shechem, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>a man found him wandering in the field and asked, “What are you looking for?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>“I am looking for my brothers,” Joseph replied. “Can you please tell me where they are pasturing their flocks?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>“They have moved on from here,” the man answered. “I heard them say, ‘Let us go to Dothan.’” So Joseph set out after his brothers and found them at Dothan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Now Joseph’s brothers saw him in the distance, and before he arrived, they plotted to kill him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>“Here comes that dreamer!” they said to one another. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>“Come now, let us kill him and throw him into one of the pits. We can say that a vicious animal has devoured him. Then we shall see what becomes of his dreams!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>When Reuben heard this, he tried to rescue Joseph from their hands. “Let us not take his life,” he said. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>“Do not shed his blood. Throw him into this pit in the wilderness, but do not lay a hand on him.” Reuben said this so that he could rescue Joseph from their hands and return him to his father. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>So when Joseph came to his brothers, they stripped him of his robe—the robe of many colors he was wearing—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>and they took him and threw him into the pit. Now the pit was empty, with no water in it. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>And as they sat down to eat a meal, they looked up and saw a caravan of Ishmaelites coming from Gilead. Their camels were carrying spices, balm, and myrrh on their way down to Egypt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Then Judah said to his brothers, “What profit will we gain if we kill our brother and cover up his blood? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Come, let us sell him to the Ishmaelites and not lay a hand on him; for he is our brother, our own flesh.” And they agreed. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>So when the Midianite traders passed by, his brothers pulled Joseph out of the pit and sold him for twenty shekels of silver <span> <a href="#genesis_37_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
to the Ishmaelites, who took him to Egypt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>When Reuben returned to the pit and saw that Joseph was not there, he tore his clothes, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>returned to his brothers, and said, “The boy is gone! What am I going to do?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_37_30_section">
<h3> Jacob Mourns Joseph
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>Then they took Joseph’s robe, slaughtered a young goat, and dipped the robe in its blood. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>They sent the robe of many colors to their father and said, “We found this. Examine it to see whether it is your son’s robe or not.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>His father recognized it and said, “It is my son’s robe! A vicious animal has devoured him. Joseph has surely been torn to pieces!” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob tore his clothes, put sackcloth around his waist, and mourned for his son many days. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>All his sons and daughters tried to comfort him, but he refused to be comforted. “No,” he said. “I will go down to Sheol mourning for my son.” So his father wept for him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_37_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>Meanwhile, the Midianites <span> <a href="#genesis_37_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
sold Joseph in Egypt to Potiphar, an officer of Pharaoh and captain of the guard. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_37_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_37_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>37:3</em>: Possibly a robe with long sleeves; also in verses 23 and 32 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_37_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>37:28</em>: 20 shekels is approximately 8 ounces or 228 grams of silver. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_37_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>37:36</em>: Hebrew the Medanites </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_38"> 38 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_38_front">
<h3> Judah and Tamar
 </h3>
<span> (1 Chronicles 2:3–4)
 </span>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>About that time, Judah left his brothers and settled near a man named Hirah, an Adullamite. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>There Judah saw the daughter of a Canaanite man named Shua, and he took her as a wife and slept with her. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>So she conceived and gave birth to a son, and Judah named him Er. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Again she conceived and gave birth to a son, and she named him Onan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Then she gave birth to another son and named him Shelah; it was at Chezib that she gave birth to him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Now Judah acquired a wife for Er, his firstborn, and her name was Tamar. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>But Er, Judah’s firstborn, was wicked in the sight of the LORD; so the LORD put him to death. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Then Judah said to Onan, “Sleep with your brother’s wife. Perform your duty as her brother-in-law and raise up offspring for your brother.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>But Onan knew that the offspring would not belong to him; so whenever he would sleep with his brother’s wife, he would spill his seed on the ground so that he would not produce offspring for his brother. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>What he did was wicked in the sight of the LORD, so He put Onan to death as well. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Then Judah said to his daughter-in-law Tamar, “Live as a widow in your father’s house until my son Shelah grows up.” For he thought, “He may die too, like his brothers.” So Tamar went to live in her father’s house. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>After a long time Judah’s wife, the daughter of Shua, died. When Judah had finished mourning, he and his friend Hirah the Adullamite went up to his sheepshearers at Timnah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>When Tamar was told, “Your father-in-law is going up to Timnah to shear his sheep,” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>she removed her widow’s garments, covered her face with a veil to disguise herself, and sat at the entrance to Enaim, which is on the way to Timnah. For she saw that although Shelah had grown up, she had not been given to him as a wife. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>When Judah saw her, he thought she was a prostitute because she had covered her face. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Not realizing that she was his daughter-in-law, he went over to her and said, “Come now, let me sleep with you.” 
“What will you give me for sleeping with you?” she inquired. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>“I will send you a young goat from my flock,” Judah answered. 
But she replied, “Only if you leave me something as a pledge until you send it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>“What pledge should I give you?” he asked. 
She answered, “Your seal and your cord, and the staff in your hand.” So he gave them to her and slept with her, and she became pregnant by him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Then Tamar got up and departed. And she removed her veil and put on her widow’s garments again. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Now when Judah sent his friend Hirah the Adullamite with the young goat to collect the items he had left with the woman, he could not find her. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>He asked the men of that place, “Where is the shrine prostitute who was beside the road at Enaim?” 
“No shrine prostitute has been here,” they answered. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>So Hirah returned to Judah and said, “I could not find her, and furthermore, the men of that place said, ‘No shrine prostitute has been here.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>“Let her keep the items,” Judah replied. “Otherwise we will become a laughingstock.<span> <a href="#genesis_38_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
After all, I did send her this young goat, but you could not find her.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>About three months later, Judah was told, “Your daughter-in-law Tamar has prostituted herself, and now she is pregnant.” 
“Bring her out!” Judah replied. “Let her be burned to death!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>As she was being brought out, Tamar sent a message to her father-in-law: “I am pregnant by the man to whom these items belong.” And she added, “Please examine them. Whose seal and cord and staff are these?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>Judah recognized the items and said, “She is more righteous than I, since I did not give her to my son Shelah.” And he did not have relations with her again. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_38_26_section">
<h3> The Birth of Perez and Zerah
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>When the time came for Tamar to give birth, there were twins in her womb. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>And as she was giving birth, one of them put out his hand; so the midwife took a scarlet thread and tied it around his wrist. “This one came out first,” she announced. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>But when he pulled his hand back and his brother came out, she said, “You have broken out first!” So he was named Perez.<span> <a href="#genesis_38_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_38_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Then his brother came out with the scarlet thread around his wrist, and he was named Zerah.<span> <a href="#genesis_38_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_38_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_38_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>38:23</em>: Or we will become despised </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_38_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>38:29</em>: Perez means breaking out. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_38_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>38:30</em>: Zerah can mean scarlet or brightness. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_39"> 39 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_39_front">
<h3> Joseph and Potiphar’s Wife
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Meanwhile, Joseph had been taken down to Egypt, where an Egyptian named Potiphar, an officer of Pharaoh and captain of the guard, bought him from the Ishmaelites who had taken him there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And the LORD was with Joseph, and he became a successful man, serving in the household of his Egyptian master. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>When his master saw that the LORD was with him and made him prosper in all he did, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Joseph found favor in his sight and became his personal attendant. 
Potiphar put him in charge of his household and entrusted him with everything he owned. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>From the time that he put Joseph in charge of his household and all he owned, the LORD blessed the Egyptian’s household on account of him. The LORD’s blessing was on everything he owned, both in his house and in his field. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>So Potiphar left all that he owned in Joseph’s care; he did not concern himself with anything except the food he ate. 
Now Joseph was well-built and handsome, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>and after some time his master’s wife cast her eyes upon Joseph and said, “Sleep with me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>But he refused. “Look,” he said to his master’s wife, “with me here, my master does not concern himself with anything in his house, and he has entrusted everything he owns to my care. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>No one in this house is greater than I am. He has withheld nothing from me except you, because you are his wife. So how could I do such a great evil and sin against God?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Although Potiphar’s wife spoke to Joseph day after day, he refused to go to bed with her or even be near her. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>One day, however, Joseph went into the house to attend to his work, and not a single household servant was inside. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>She grabbed Joseph by his cloak and said, “Sleep with me!” But leaving his cloak in her hand, he escaped and ran outside. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_39_12_section">
<h3> Joseph Falsely Imprisoned
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>When she saw that he had left his cloak in her hand and had run out of the house, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>she called her household servants. “Look,” she said, “this Hebrew has been brought to us to make sport of us. He came to me so he could sleep with me, but I screamed as loud as I could. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>When he heard me scream for help, he left his cloak beside me and ran out of the house.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>So Potiphar’s wife kept Joseph’s cloak beside her until his master came home. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Then she told him the same story: “The Hebrew slave you brought us came to me to make sport of me, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>but when I screamed for help, he left his cloak beside me and ran out of the house.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>When his master heard the story his wife told him, saying, “This is what your slave did to me,” he burned with anger. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>So Joseph’s master took him and had him thrown into the prison where the king’s prisoners were confined. 
While Joseph was there in the prison, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>the LORD was with him and extended kindness to him, granting him favor in the eyes of the prison warden. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And the warden put all the prisoners under Joseph’s care, so that he was responsible for all that was done in the prison. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_39_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>The warden did not concern himself with anything under Joseph’s care, because the LORD was with Joseph and gave him success in whatever he did. 
</div></div>

</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_40"> 40 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_40_front">
<h3> The Cupbearer and the Baker
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Some time later, the king’s cupbearer and baker offended their master, the king of Egypt. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh was angry with his two officers, the chief cupbearer and the chief baker, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>and imprisoned them in the house of the captain of the guard, the same prison where Joseph was confined. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>The captain of the guard assigned them to Joseph, and he became their personal attendant. 
After they had been in custody for some time, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>both of these men—the Egyptian king’s cupbearer and baker, who were being held in the prison—had a dream on the same night, and each dream had its own meaning. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>When Joseph came to them in the morning, he saw that they were distraught. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>So he asked the officials of Pharaoh who were in custody with him in his master’s house, “Why are your faces so downcast today?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>“We both had dreams,” they replied, “but there is no one to interpret them.” 
Then Joseph said to them, “Don’t interpretations belong to God? Tell me your dreams.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>So the chief cupbearer told Joseph his dream: “In my dream there was a vine before me, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>and on the vine were three branches. As it budded, its blossoms opened and its clusters ripened into grapes. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh’s cup was in my hand, and I took the grapes, squeezed them into his cup, and placed the cup in his hand.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Joseph replied, “This is the interpretation: The three branches are three days. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Within three days Pharaoh will lift up your head and restore your position. You will put Pharaoh’s cup in his hand, just as you did when you were his cupbearer. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>But when it goes well for you, please remember me and show me kindness by mentioning me to Pharaoh, that he might bring me out of this prison. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>For I was kidnapped from the land of the Hebrews, and even here I have done nothing for which they should have put me in this dungeon.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>When the chief baker saw that the interpretation was favorable, he said to Joseph, “I too had a dream: There were three baskets of white bread on my head. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>In the top basket were all sorts of baked goods for Pharaoh, but the birds were eating them out of the basket on my head.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Joseph replied, “This is the interpretation: The three baskets are three days. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Within three days Pharaoh will lift off your head and hang you on a tree.<span> <a href="#genesis_40_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
Then the birds will eat the flesh of your body.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>On the third day, which was Pharaoh’s birthday, he held a feast for all his officials, and in their presence he lifted up the heads of the chief cupbearer and the chief baker. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh restored the chief cupbearer to his position, so that he once again placed the cup in Pharaoh’s hand. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>But Pharaoh hanged the chief baker, just as Joseph had described to them in his interpretation.<span> <a href="#genesis_40_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_40_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>The chief cupbearer, however, did not remember Joseph; he forgot all about him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_40_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_40_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>40:19</em>: Or and impale you on a pole; similarly in verse 22 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_40_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>40:22</em>: Literally had interpreted to them </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_41"> 41 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_41_front">
<h3> The Dreams of Pharaoh
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>After two full years had passed, Pharaoh had a dream: He was standing beside the Nile, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>when seven cows, sleek and well-fed, came up from the river and began to graze among the reeds. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>After them, seven other cows, sickly and thin, came up from the Nile and stood beside the well-fed cows on the bank of the river. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>And the cows that were sickly and thin devoured the seven sleek, well-fed cows. 
Then Pharaoh woke up, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>but he fell back asleep and dreamed a second time: Seven heads of grain, plump and ripe, came up on one stalk. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>After them, seven other heads of grain sprouted, thin and scorched by the east wind. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And the thin heads of grain swallowed up the seven plump, ripe ones. Then Pharaoh awoke and realized it was a dream. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>In the morning his spirit was troubled, so he summoned all the magicians and wise men of Egypt. Pharaoh told them his dreams, but no one could interpret them for him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Then the chief cupbearer said to Pharaoh, “Today I recall my failures. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh was once angry with his servants, and he put me and the chief baker in the custody of the captain of the guard. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>One night both the chief baker and I had dreams, and each dream had its own meaning. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Now a young Hebrew was there with us, a servant of the captain of the guard. We told him our dreams and he interpreted them for us individually. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>And it happened to us just as he had interpreted: I was restored to my position, and the other man was hanged.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_41_13_section">
<h3> Joseph Interprets Pharaoh’s Dreams
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>So Pharaoh sent for Joseph, who was quickly brought out of the dungeon. After he had shaved and changed his clothes, he went in before Pharaoh. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh said to Joseph, “I had a dream, and no one can interpret it. But I have heard it said of you that when you hear a dream you can interpret it.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>“I myself cannot do it,” Joseph replied, “but God will give Pharaoh a sound answer.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Then Pharaoh said to Joseph: “In my dream I was standing on the bank of the Nile, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>when seven cows, well-fed and sleek, came up from the river and began to graze among the reeds. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>After them, seven other cows—sickly, ugly, and thin—came up. I have never seen such ugly cows in all the land of Egypt! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Then the thin, ugly cows devoured the seven well-fed cows that were there first. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>When they had devoured them, however, no one could tell that they had done so; their appearance was as ugly as it had been before. Then I awoke. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>In my dream I also saw seven heads of grain, plump and ripe, growing on a single stalk. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>After them, seven other heads of grain sprouted—withered, thin, and scorched by the east wind. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>And the thin heads of grain swallowed the seven plump ones. 
I told this dream to the magicians, but no one could explain it to me.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>At this, Joseph said to Pharaoh, “The dreams of Pharaoh are one and the same. God has revealed to Pharaoh what He is about to do. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>The seven good cows are seven years, and the seven ripe heads of grain are seven years. The dreams have the same meaning. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Moreover, the seven thin, ugly cows that came up after them are seven years, and so are the seven worthless heads of grain scorched by the east wind—they are seven years of famine. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>It is just as I said to Pharaoh: God has shown Pharaoh what He is about to do. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Behold, seven years of great abundance are coming throughout the land of Egypt, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>but seven years of famine will follow them. Then all the abundance in the land of Egypt will be forgotten, and the famine will devastate the land. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>The abundance in the land will not be remembered, since the famine that follows it will be so severe. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>Moreover, because the dream was given to Pharaoh in two versions, the matter has been decreed by God, and He will carry it out shortly. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>Now, therefore, Pharaoh should look for a discerning and wise man and set him over the land of Egypt. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>Let Pharaoh take action and appoint commissioners over the land to take a fifth of the harvest <span> <a href="#genesis_41_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
of Egypt during the seven years of abundance. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>Under the authority of Pharaoh, let them collect all the excess food from these good years, that they may come and lay up the grain to be preserved as food in the cities. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>This food will be a reserve for the land during the seven years of famine to come upon the land of Egypt. Then the country will not perish in the famine.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_41_36_section">
<h3> Joseph Given Charge of Egypt
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>This proposal pleased Pharaoh and all his officials. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>So Pharaoh asked them, “Can we find anyone like this man, in whom the Spirit of God <span> <a href="#genesis_41_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
abides?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_39"> 39 </a> 
<div>Then Pharaoh said to Joseph, “Since God has made all this known to you, there is no one as discerning and wise as you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_40"> 40 </a> 
<div>You shall be in charge of my house, and all my people are to obey your commands. Only with regard to the throne will I be greater than you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_41"> 41 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh also told Joseph, “I hereby place you over all the land of Egypt.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_42"> 42 </a> 
<div>Then Pharaoh removed the signet ring from his finger, put it on Joseph’s finger, clothed him in garments of fine linen, and placed a gold chain around his neck. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_43"> 43 </a> 
<div>He had Joseph ride in his second chariot, with men calling out before him, “Bow the knee!”<span> <a href="#genesis_41_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
So he placed him over all the land of Egypt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_44"> 44 </a> 
<div>And Pharaoh declared to Joseph, “I am Pharaoh, but without your permission, no one in all the land of Egypt shall lift his hand or foot.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_45"> 45 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh gave Joseph the name Zaphenath-paneah,<span> <a href="#genesis_41_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
 and he gave him Asenath daughter of Potiphera, priest of On,<span> <a href="#genesis_41_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
to be his wife. And Joseph took charge of all the land of Egypt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_41_45_section">
<h3> The Seven Years of Plenty
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_46"> 46 </a> 
<div>Now Joseph was thirty years old when he entered the service of Pharaoh king of Egypt. And Joseph left Pharaoh’s presence and traveled throughout the land of Egypt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_47"> 47 </a> 
<div>During the seven years of abundance, the land brought forth bountifully. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_48"> 48 </a> 
<div>During those seven years, Joseph collected all the excess food in the land of Egypt and stored it in the cities. In every city he laid up the food from the fields around it. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_49"> 49 </a> 
<div>So Joseph stored up grain in such abundance, like the sand of the sea, that he stopped keeping track of it; for it was beyond measure. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_50"> 50 </a> 
<div>Before the years of famine arrived, two sons were born to Joseph by Asenath daughter of Potiphera, priest of On. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_51"> 51 </a> 
<div>Joseph named the firstborn Manasseh,<span> <a href="#genesis_41_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
saying, “God has made me forget all my hardship and all my father’s household.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_52"> 52 </a> 
<div>And the second son he named Ephraim,<span> <a href="#genesis_41_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>
saying, “God has made me fruitful in the land of my affliction.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_41_52_section">
<h3> The Famine Begins
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_53"> 53 </a> 
<div>When the seven years of abundance in the land of Egypt came to an end, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_54"> 54 </a> 
<div>the seven years of famine began, just as Joseph had said. And although there was famine in every country, there was food throughout the land of Egypt. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_55"> 55 </a> 
<div>When extreme hunger came to all the land of Egypt and the people cried out to Pharaoh for food, he told all the Egyptians, “Go to Joseph and do whatever he tells you.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_56"> 56 </a> 
<div>When the famine had spread over all the land, Joseph opened up all the storehouses and sold grain to the Egyptians; for the famine was severe in the land of Egypt. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_41_57"> 57 </a> 
<div>And every nation came to Joseph in Egypt to buy grain, because the famine was severe over all the earth. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_41_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_41_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>41:34</em>: See LXX; MT a fifth from the land </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_41_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>41:38</em>: Or the spirit of the gods </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_41_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>41:43</em>: That is, “Abrek,” probably an Egyptian word that sounds similar to the Hebrew for kneel </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_41_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>41:45</em>: Zaphenath-paneah probably means Revealer of Mysteries or God speaks and lives. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_41_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>41:45</em>: That is, Heliopolis, as in LXX; also in verse 50 </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_41_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>41:51</em>: Manasseh sounds like the Hebrew for making to forget. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_41_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>41:52</em>: Ephraim sounds like the Hebrew for making fruitful or twice fruitful. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_42"> 42 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_42_front">
<h3> Joseph’s Brothers Sent to Egypt
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>When Jacob learned that there was grain in Egypt, he said to his sons, “Why are you staring at one another?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>“Look,” he added, “I have heard that there is grain in Egypt. Go down there and buy some for us, so that we may live and not die.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>So ten of Joseph’s brothers went down to buy grain from Egypt. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>But Jacob did not send Joseph’s brother Benjamin with his brothers, for he said, “I am afraid that harm might befall him.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>So the sons of Israel were among those who came to buy grain, since the famine had also spread to the land of Canaan. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Now Joseph was the ruler of the land; he was the one who sold grain to all its people. So when his brothers arrived, they bowed down before him with their faces to the ground. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>And when Joseph saw his brothers, he recognized them, but he treated them as strangers and spoke harshly to them. “Where have you come from?” he asked. 
“From the land of Canaan,” they replied. “We are here to buy food.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Although Joseph recognized his brothers, they did not recognize him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Joseph remembered his dreams about them and said, “You are spies! You have come to see if our land is vulnerable.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>“Not so, my lord,” they replied. “Your servants have come to buy food. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>We are all sons of one man. Your servants are honest men, not spies.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>“No,” he told them. “You have come to see if our land is vulnerable.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>But they answered, “Your servants are twelve brothers, the sons of one man in the land of Canaan. The youngest is now with our father, and one is no more.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph declared, “Just as I said, you are spies! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>And this is how you will be tested: As surely as Pharaoh lives, you shall not leave this place unless your youngest brother comes here. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Send one of your number to get your brother; the rest of you will be confined so that the truth of your words may be tested. If they are untrue, then as surely as Pharaoh lives, you are spies!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>So Joseph imprisoned them for three days, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>and on the third day he said to them, “I fear God. So do this and you will live: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>If you are honest, leave one of your brothers in custody while the rest of you go and take back grain to relieve the hunger of your households. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Then bring your youngest brother to me so that your words can be verified, that you may not die.” 
And to this they consented. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Then they said to one another, “Surely we are being punished because of our brother. We saw his anguish when he pleaded with us, but we would not listen. That is why this distress has come upon us.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And Reuben responded, “Didn’t I tell you not to sin against the boy? But you would not listen. Now we must account for his blood!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>They did not realize that Joseph understood them, since there was an interpreter between them. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>And he turned away from them and wept. When he turned back and spoke to them, he took Simeon from them and had him bound before their eyes. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_42_24_section">
<h3> Joseph’s Brothers Return to Canaan
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph gave orders to fill their bags with grain, to return each man’s silver <span> <a href="#genesis_42_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
to his sack, and to give them provisions for their journey. This order was carried out, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>and they loaded the grain on their donkeys and departed. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>At the place where they lodged for the night, one of them opened his sack to get feed for his donkey, and he saw his silver in the mouth of the sack. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>“My silver has been returned!” he said to his brothers. “It is here in my sack.” 
Their hearts sank, and trembling, they turned to one another and said, “What is this that God has done to us?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>When they reached their father Jacob in the land of Canaan, they described to him all that had happened to them: 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>“The man who is lord of the land spoke harshly to us and accused us of spying on the country. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>But we told him, ‘We are honest men, not spies. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>We are twelve brothers, sons of one father. One is no more, and the youngest is now with our father in the land of Canaan.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>Then the man who is lord of the land said to us, ‘This is how I will know whether you are honest: Leave one brother with me, take food to relieve the hunger of your households, and go. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>But bring your youngest brother back to me so I will know that you are not spies but honest men. Then I will give your brother back to you, and you can trade in the land.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_35"> 35 </a> 
<div>As they began emptying their sacks, there in each man’s sack was his bag of silver! And when they and their father saw the bags of silver, they were dismayed. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_36"> 36 </a> 
<div>Their father Jacob said to them, “You have deprived me of my sons. Joseph is gone and Simeon is no more. Now you want to take Benjamin. Everything is going against me!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_37"> 37 </a> 
<div>Then Reuben said to his father, “You may kill my two sons if I fail to bring him back to you. Put him in my care, and I will return him.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_42_38"> 38 </a> 
<div>But Jacob replied, “My son will not go down there with you, for his brother is dead, and he alone is left. If any harm comes to him on your journey, you will bring my gray hair down to Sheol in sorrow.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_42_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_42_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>42:25</em>: Or money; here and throughout chapters 42–44 </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_43"> 43 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_43_front">
<h3> The Return to Egypt with Benjamin
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Now the famine was still severe in the land. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>So when Jacob’s sons had eaten all the grain they had brought from Egypt, their father said to them, “Go back and buy us a little more food.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>But Judah replied, “The man solemnly warned us, ‘You will not see my face again unless your brother is with you.’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>If you will send our brother with us, we will go down and buy food for you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>But if you will not send him, we will not go; for the man told us, ‘You will not see my face again unless your brother is with you.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>“Why did you bring this trouble upon me?” Israel asked. “Why did you tell the man you had another brother?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>They replied, “The man questioned us in detail about ourselves and our family: ‘Is your father still alive? Do you have another brother?’ And we answered him accordingly. How could we possibly know that he would say, ‘Bring your brother here’?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>And Judah said to his father Israel, “Send the boy with me, and we will go at once, so that we may live and not die—neither we, nor you, nor our children. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>I will guarantee his safety. You may hold me personally responsible. If I do not bring him back and set him before you, then may I bear the guilt before you all my life. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>If we had not delayed, we could have come and gone twice by now.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>Then their father Israel said to them, “If it must be so, then do this: Put some of the best products of the land in your packs and carry them down as a gift for the man—a little balm and a little honey, spices and myrrh, pistachios and almonds. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Take double the silver with you so that you may return the silver that was put back into the mouths of your sacks. Perhaps it was a mistake. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Take your brother as well, and return to the man at once. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>May God Almighty <span> <a href="#genesis_43_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
grant you mercy before the man, that he may release your other brother along with Benjamin. As for me, if I am bereaved, I am bereaved.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>So the men took these gifts, along with double the amount of silver, and Benjamin as well. Then they hurried down to Egypt and stood before Joseph. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_43_15_section">
<h3> Joseph’s Hospitality to His Brothers
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>When Joseph saw Benjamin with his brothers, he said to the steward of his house, “Take these men to my house. Slaughter an animal and prepare it, for they shall dine with me at noon.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>The man did as Joseph had commanded and took the brothers to Joseph’s house. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>But the brothers were frightened that they had been taken to Joseph’s house. “We have been brought here because of the silver that was returned in our bags the first time,” they said. “They intend to overpower us and take us as slaves, along with our donkeys.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>So they approached Joseph’s steward and spoke to him at the entrance to the house. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>“Please, sir,” they said, “we really did come down here the first time to buy food. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>But when we came to the place we lodged for the night, we opened our sacks and, behold, each of us found his silver in the mouth of his sack! It was the full amount of our silver, and we have brought it back with us. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>We have brought additional silver with us to buy food. We do not know who put our silver in our sacks.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>“It is fine,” said the steward. “Do not be afraid. Your God, the God of your father, gave you the treasure that was in your sacks. I received your silver.” Then he brought Simeon out to them. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>And the steward took the men into Joseph’s house, gave them water to wash their feet, and provided food for their donkeys. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Since the brothers had been told that they were going to eat a meal there, they prepared their gift for Joseph’s arrival at noon. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>When Joseph came home, they presented him with the gifts they had brought, and they bowed to the ground before him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>He asked if they were well, and then he asked, “How is your elderly father you told me about? Is he still alive?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>“Your servant our father is well,” they answered. “He is still alive.” And they bowed down to honor him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>When Joseph looked up and saw his brother Benjamin, his own mother’s son, he asked, “Is this your youngest brother, the one you told me about?” Then he declared, “May God be gracious to you, my son.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Joseph hurried out because he was moved to tears for his brother, and he went to a private room to weep. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>Then he washed his face and came back out. Regaining his composure, he said, “Serve the meal.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>They separately served Joseph, his brothers, and the Egyptians. They ate separately because the Egyptians would not eat with the Hebrews, since that was detestable to them. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>They were seated before Joseph in order by age, from the firstborn to the youngest, and the men looked at one another in astonishment. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_43_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>When the portions were served to them from Joseph’s table, Benjamin’s portion was five times larger than any of the others. So they feasted and drank freely with Joseph. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_43_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_43_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>43:14</em>: Hebrew El-Shaddai </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_44"> 44 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_44_front">
<h3> Benjamin and the Silver Cup
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph instructed his steward: “Fill the men’s sacks with as much food as they can carry, and put each one’s silver in the mouth of his sack. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Put my cup, the silver one, in the mouth of the youngest one’s sack, along with the silver for his grain.” 
So the steward did as Joseph had instructed. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>At daybreak, the men were sent on their way with their donkeys. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>They had not gone far from the city when Joseph told his steward, “Pursue the men at once, and when you overtake them, ask, ‘Why have you repaid good with evil?<span> <a href="#genesis_44_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Is this not the cup <span> <a href="#genesis_44_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
my master drinks from and uses for divination? What you have done is wicked!’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>When the steward overtook them, he relayed these words to them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>“Why does my lord say these things?” they asked. “Your servants could not possibly do such a thing. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>We even brought back to you from the land of Canaan the silver we found in the mouths of our sacks. Why would we steal silver or gold from your master’s house? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>If any of your servants is found to have it, he must die, and the rest will become slaves of my lord.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>“As you say,” replied the steward. “But only the one who is found with the cup will be my slave, and the rest of you shall be free of blame.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>So each one quickly lowered his sack to the ground and opened it. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>The steward searched, beginning with the oldest and ending with the youngest—and the cup was found in Benjamin’s sack. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Then they all tore their clothes, loaded their donkeys, and returned to the city. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>When Judah and his brothers arrived at Joseph’s house, he was still there, and they fell to the ground before him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>“What is this deed you have done?” Joseph declared. “Do you not know that a man like me can surely divine the truth?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>“What can we say to my lord?” Judah replied. “How can we plead? How can we justify ourselves? God has exposed the iniquity of your servants. We are now my lord’s slaves—both we and the one who was found with the cup.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>But Joseph replied, “Far be it from me to do this. The man who was found with the cup will be my slave. The rest of you may return to your father in peace.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_44_17_section">
<h3> Judah Pleads for Benjamin
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Then Judah approached Joseph and said, “Sir, please let your servant speak personally to my lord. Do not be angry with your servant, for you are equal to Pharaoh himself. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>My lord asked his servants, ‘Do you have a father or a brother?’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>And we answered, ‘We have an elderly father and a younger brother, the child of his old age. The boy’s brother is dead. He is the only one of his mother’s sons left, and his father loves him.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Then you told your servants, ‘Bring him down to me so that I can see him for myself.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>So we said to my lord, ‘The boy cannot leave his father. If he were to leave, his father would die.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>But you said to your servants, ‘Unless your younger brother comes down with you, you will not see my face again.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Now when we returned to your servant my father, we relayed your words to him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>Then our father said, ‘Go back and buy us some food.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>But we answered, ‘We cannot go down there unless our younger brother goes with us. So if our younger brother is not with us, we cannot see the man.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>And your servant my father said to us, ‘You know that my wife bore me two sons. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>When one of them was gone, I said: “Surely he has been torn to pieces.” And I have not seen him since. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Now if you also take this one from me and harm comes to him, you will bring my gray hair down to Sheol in sorrow.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>So if the boy is not with us when I return to your servant, and if my father, whose life is wrapped up in the boy’s life, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>sees that the boy is not with us, he will die. Then your servants will have brought the gray hair of your servant our father down to Sheol in sorrow. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_32"> 32 </a>
