<div>Indeed, your servant guaranteed the boy’s safety to my father, saying, ‘If I do not return him to you, I will bear the guilt before you, my father, all my life.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>Now please let your servant stay here as my lord’s slave in place of the boy. Let him return with his brothers. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_44_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>For how can I go back to my father without the boy? I could not bear to see the misery that would overwhelm him.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_44_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_44_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>44:4</em>: LXX includes Why have you stolen my silver cup? </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_44_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>44:5</em>: Hebrew Is it not this which </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_45"> 45 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_45_front">
<h3> Joseph Reveals His Identity
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph could no longer control himself before all his attendants, and he cried out, “Send everyone away from me!” 
So none of them were with Joseph when he made himself known to his brothers. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>But he wept so loudly that the Egyptians heard him, and Pharaoh’s household soon heard of it. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Joseph said to his brothers, “I am Joseph! Is my father still alive?” 
But they were unable to answer him, because they were terrified in his presence. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph said to his brothers, “Please come near me.” And they did so. 
“I am Joseph, your brother,” he said, “the one you sold into Egypt! 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And now, do not be distressed or angry with yourselves that you sold me into this place, because it was to save lives that God sent me before you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>For the famine has covered the land these two years, and there will be five more years without plowing or harvesting. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>God sent me before you to preserve you as a remnant on the earth and to save your lives by a great deliverance.<span> <a href="#genesis_45_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Therefore it was not you who sent me here, but God, who has made me a father to Pharaoh—lord of all his household and ruler over all the land of Egypt. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_45_8_section">
<h3> Joseph Sends for His Father
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Now return quickly to my father and tell him, ‘This is what your son Joseph says: God has made me lord of all Egypt. Come down to me without delay. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>You shall settle in the land of Goshen and be near me—you and your children and grandchildren, your flocks and herds, and everything you own. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>And there I will provide for you, because there will be five more years of famine. Otherwise, you and your household and everything you own will come to destitution.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Behold! You and my brother Benjamin can see that I, Joseph, am the one speaking with you. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Tell my father about all my splendor in Egypt and everything you have seen. And bring my father down here quickly.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph threw his arms around his brother Benjamin and wept, and Benjamin wept as they embraced. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Joseph kissed each of his brothers as he wept over them. And afterward his brothers talked with him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_45_15_section">
<h3> Pharaoh Invites Jacob to Egypt
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>When the news reached Pharaoh’s house that Joseph’s brothers had come, Pharaoh and his servants were pleased. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh said to Joseph, “Tell your brothers, ‘Do as follows: Load your animals and return to the land of Canaan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>Then bring your father and your families and return to me. I will give you the best of the land of Egypt, and you shall eat from the fat of the land.’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>You are also directed to tell them: ‘Take wagons from the land of Egypt for your young children and your wives, and bring your father and come back. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>But pay no regard to your belongings, for the best of all the land of Egypt is yours.’” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>So the sons of Israel did as they were told. Joseph gave them wagons as Pharaoh had instructed, and he also gave them provisions for their journey. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>He gave new garments to each of them, but to Benjamin he gave three hundred shekels of silver <span> <a href="#genesis_45_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
and five sets of clothes. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>And he sent to his father the following: ten donkeys loaded with the best of Egypt, and ten female donkeys loaded with grain and bread and provisions for his father’s journey. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph sent his brothers on their way, and as they were leaving, he said to them, “Do not quarrel on the way!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_45_24_section">
<h3> The Revival of Jacob
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>So the brothers went up out of Egypt and came to their father Jacob in the land of Canaan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>“Joseph is still alive,” they said, “and he is ruler over all the land of Egypt!” 
But Jacob was stunned, for he did not believe them. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>However, when they relayed all that Joseph had told them, and when he saw the wagons that Joseph had sent to carry him back, the spirit of their father Jacob was revived. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_45_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>“Enough!” declared Israel. “My son Joseph is still alive! I will go to see him before I die.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_45_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_45_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>45:7</em>: Or and to keep you alive as a great band of survivors </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_45_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>45:22</em>: 300 shekels is approximately 7.5 pounds or 3.4 kilograms of silver. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_46"> 46 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_46_front">
<h3> Jacob’s Journey to Egypt
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>So Israel set out with all that he had, and when he came to Beersheba, he offered sacrifices to the God of his father Isaac. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And that night God spoke to Israel in a vision: “Jacob, Jacob!” He said. 
“Here I am,” replied Jacob. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>“I am God,” He said, “the God of your father. Do not be afraid to go down to Egypt, for I will make you into a great nation there. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>I will go down with you to Egypt, and I will surely bring you back. And Joseph’s own hands will close your eyes.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob departed from Beersheba, and the sons of Israel took their father Jacob in the wagons Pharaoh had sent to carry him, along with their children and wives. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>They also took the livestock and possessions they had acquired in the land of Canaan, and Jacob and all his offspring went to Egypt. 
<span> (Exodus 1:1–7)
 </span>
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_46_6_section">
<h3> Those Who Went to Egypt
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Jacob took with him to Egypt his sons and grandsons, and his daughters and granddaughters—all his offspring. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Now these are the names of the sons of Israel (Jacob and his descendants) who went to Egypt: Reuben, Jacob’s firstborn. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>The sons of Reuben: Hanoch, Pallu, Hezron, and Carmi. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>The sons of Simeon: Jemuel,<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
 Jamin, Ohad, Jachin, Zohar,<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
and Shaul the son of a Canaanite woman. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>The sons of Levi: Gershon, Kohath, and Merari. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>The sons of Judah: Er, Onan, Shelah, Perez, and Zerah; but Er and Onan died in the land of Canaan. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>The sons of Issachar: Tola, Puvah,<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
 Job,<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
and Shimron. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>The sons of Zebulun: Sered, Elon, and Jahleel. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Leah born to Jacob in Paddan-aram,<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>
in addition to his daughter Dinah. The total number of sons and daughters was thirty-three. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>The sons of Gad: Ziphion,<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>
 Haggi, Shuni, Ezbon, Eri, Arodi,<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>
and Areli. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>The children of Asher: Imnah, Ishvah, Ishvi, Beriah, and their sister Serah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Jacob born to Zilpah—whom Laban gave to his daughter Leah—sixteen in all. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>The sons of Jacob’s wife Rachel: Joseph and Benjamin. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Manasseh and Ephraim were born to Joseph in the land of Egypt by Asenath daughter of Potiphera, priest of On.<span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_8">[8]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>The sons of Benjamin: Bela, Becher, Ashbel, Gera, Naaman, Ehi, Rosh, Muppim, Huppim, and Ard. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Rachel born to Jacob—fourteen in all. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>The son of Dan: Hushim. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>The sons of Naphtali: Jahzeel, Guni, Jezer, and Shillem. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>These are the sons of Jacob born to Bilhah, whom Laban gave to his daughter Rachel—seven in all. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>All those belonging to Jacob who came to Egypt—his direct descendants, besides the wives of Jacob’s sons—numbered sixty-six persons. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>And with the two sons <span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_9">[9]</a> </span>
 who had been born to Joseph in Egypt, the members of Jacob’s family who went to Egypt were seventy <span> <a href="#genesis_46_footnote_10">[10]</a> </span>
in all. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_46_27_section">
<h3> Jacob Arrives in Egypt
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>Now Jacob had sent Judah ahead of him to Joseph to get directions to Goshen. When Jacob’s family arrived in the land of Goshen, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Joseph prepared his chariot and went there to meet his father Israel. Joseph presented himself to him, embraced him, and wept profusely. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>Then Israel said to Joseph, “Finally I can die, now that I have seen your face and know that you are still alive!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>Joseph said to his brothers and to his father’s household, “I will go up and inform Pharaoh: ‘My brothers and my father’s household from the land of Canaan have come to me. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>The men are shepherds; they raise livestock, and they have brought their flocks and herds and all that they own.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>When Pharaoh summons you and asks, ‘What is your occupation?’ 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_46_34"> 34 </a> 
<div>you are to say, ‘Your servants have raised livestock ever since our youth—both we and our fathers.’ Then you will be allowed to settle in the land of Goshen, since all shepherds are detestable to the Egyptians.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_46_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>46:10</em>: Jemuel is another name for Nemuel; see Numbers 26:12. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>46:10</em>: Zohar is a variant of Zerah; see Numbers 26:13 and 1 Chronicles 4:24. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>46:13</em>: Hebrew; SP and Syriac Puah; see 1 Chronicles 7:1. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>46:13</em>: Hebrew; SP and some LXX manuscripts Jashub; see Numbers 26:24 and 1 Chronicles 7:1. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>46:15</em>: That is, northwest Mesopotamia </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>46:16</em>: SP and LXX Zephon; see also Numbers 26:15. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>46:16</em>: Arodi is a variant of Arod; see Numbers 26:17. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_8>[8]</a> <em>46:20</em>: That is, Heliopolis, as in LXX </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_9>[9]</a> <em>46:27</em>: Hebrew; LXX the nine sons, probably including Joseph’s grandsons through Ephraim and Manasseh; see 1 Chronicles 7:14–29. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_46_footnote_10>[10]</a> <em>46:27</em>: Hebrew (see also Exodus 1:5); LXX (see also Acts 7:14) seventy-five </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_47"> 47 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_47_front">
<h3> Jacob Settles in Goshen
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>So Joseph went and told Pharaoh: “My father and my brothers, with their flocks and herds and all they own, have come from the land of Canaan and are now in Goshen.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And he chose five of his brothers and presented them before Pharaoh. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>“What is your occupation?” Pharaoh asked Joseph’s brothers. 
“Your servants are shepherds,” they replied, “both we and our fathers.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Then they said to Pharaoh, “We have come to live in the land for a time, because there is no pasture for the flocks of your servants, since the famine in the land of Canaan has been severe. So now, please allow your servants to settle in the land of Goshen.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh said to Joseph, “Now that your father and brothers have come to you, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>the land of Egypt is before you; settle your father and brothers in the best part of the land. They may dwell in the land of Goshen. And if you know of any talented men among them, put them in charge of my own livestock.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph brought in his father Jacob and presented him before Pharaoh, and Jacob blessed Pharaoh. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>“How many years have you lived?” Pharaoh asked. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>“My travels <span> <a href="#genesis_47_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
have lasted 130 years,” Jacob replied. “My years have been few and hard, and they have not matched the years of the travels of my fathers.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob blessed Pharaoh and departed from his presence. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>So Joseph settled his father and brothers in the land of Egypt and gave them property in the best part of the land, the district of Rameses, as Pharaoh had commanded. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Joseph also provided his father and brothers and all his father’s household with food for their families. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_47_12_section">
<h3> The Famine Continues
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>There was no food, however, in all that region, because the famine was so severe; the lands of Egypt and Canaan had been exhausted by the famine. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Joseph collected all the money to be found in the land of Egypt and the land of Canaan in exchange for the grain they were buying, and he brought it into Pharaoh’s palace. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>When the money from the lands of Egypt and Canaan was gone, all the Egyptians came to Joseph and said, “Give us food. Why should we die before your eyes? For our funds have run out!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>“Then bring me your livestock,” said Joseph. “Since the money is gone, I will sell you food in exchange for your livestock.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>So they brought their livestock to Joseph, and he gave them food in exchange for their horses, their flocks and herds, and their donkeys. Throughout that year he provided them with food in exchange for all their livestock. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>When that year was over, they came to him the second year and said, “We cannot hide from our lord that our money is gone and all our livestock belongs to you. There is nothing left for our lord except our bodies and our land. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Why should we perish before your eyes—we and our land as well? Purchase us and our land in exchange for food. Then we, along with our land, will be slaves to Pharaoh. Give us seed that we may live and not die, and that the land may not become desolate.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>So Joseph acquired for Pharaoh all the land in Egypt; the Egyptians, one and all, sold their fields because the famine was so severe upon them. The land became Pharaoh’s, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>and Joseph reduced the people to servitude <span> <a href="#genesis_47_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
from one end of Egypt to the other. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>However, he did not acquire the priests’ portion of the land, for it had been given to them by Pharaoh. They ate the rations that Pharaoh supplied; so they did not sell their land. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph said to the people, “Now that I have acquired you and your land for Pharaoh this day, here is seed for you to sow in the land. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>At harvest time, you are to give a fifth of it to Pharaoh, and four-fifths will be yours as seed for the field and food for yourselves and your households and children.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>“You have saved our lives,” they said. “We have found favor in our lord’s eyes, and we will be Pharaoh’s servants.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>So Joseph established a law that a fifth of the produce belongs to Pharaoh, and it is in effect in the land of Egypt to this day. Only the priests’ land does not belong to Pharaoh. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_47_26_section">
<h3> The Israelites Prosper in Goshen
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Now the Israelites settled in the land of Egypt, in the region of Goshen. They acquired property there and became fruitful and increased greatly in number. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>And Jacob lived in the land of Egypt seventeen years, and the length of his life was 147 years. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>When the time drew near for Israel to die, he called his son Joseph and said to him, “If I have found favor in your eyes, put your hand under my thigh and promise to show me kindness and faithfulness. Do not bury me in Egypt, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>but when I lie down with my fathers, carry me out of Egypt and bury me with them.” 
Joseph answered, “I will do as you have requested.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_47_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>“Swear to me,” Jacob said. 
So Joseph swore to him, and Israel bowed in worship at the head of his bed.<span> <a href="#genesis_47_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_47_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_47_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>47:9</em>: Hebrew sojourns; twice in this verse </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_47_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>47:21</em>: SP, LXX, Vulgate; Hebrew removed the people to the cities </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_47_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>47:31</em>: Hebrew; LXX Israel worshiped as he leaned on the top of his staff; see Hebrews 11:21. </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_48"> 48 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_48_front">
<h3> Jacob Blesses Ephraim and Manasseh
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Some time later Joseph was told, “Your father is ill.” So he set out with his two sons, Manasseh and Ephraim. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>When Jacob was told, “Your son Joseph has come to you,” Israel rallied his strength and sat up in bed. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Jacob said to Joseph, “God Almighty <span> <a href="#genesis_48_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
appeared to me at Luz in the land of Canaan, and there He blessed me 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>and told me, ‘Behold, I will make you fruitful and multiply you; I will make you a multitude of peoples, and will give this land to your descendants after you as an everlasting possession.’ 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>And now your two sons born to you in Egypt before I came to you here shall be reckoned as mine; Ephraim and Manasseh shall be mine, just as Reuben and Simeon are mine. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Any children born to you after them shall be yours, and they shall be called by the names of their brothers in the territory they inherit. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Now as for me, when I was returning from Paddan,<span> <a href="#genesis_48_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
to my sorrow Rachel died along the way in the land of Canaan, some distance from Ephrath. So I buried her there beside the road to Ephrath” (that is, Bethlehem). 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>When Israel saw the sons of Joseph, he asked, “Who are these?” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Joseph said to his father, “They are the sons God has given me in this place.” 
So Jacob said, “Please bring them to me, that I may bless them.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>Now Israel’s eyesight was poor because of old age; he could hardly see. Joseph brought his sons to him, and his father kissed them and embraced them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>“I never expected to see your face again,” Israel said to Joseph, “but now God has let me see your children as well.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph removed his sons from his father’s knees and bowed facedown. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>And Joseph took both of them—with Ephraim in his right hand toward Israel’s left hand, and Manasseh in his left hand toward Israel’s right hand—and brought them close to him. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>But Israel stretched out his right hand and put it on the head of Ephraim, the younger; and crossing his hands, he put his left on Manasseh’s head, although Manasseh was the firstborn. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>Then he blessed Joseph and said: 
“May the God before whom my fathers Abraham and Isaac walked, 
the God who has been my shepherd all my life to this day, 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>the angel <span> <a href="#genesis_48_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>
who has redeemed me from all harm—
may He bless these boys. 
And may they be called by my name 
and the names of my fathers Abraham and Isaac, 
and may they grow into a multitude upon the earth.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>When Joseph saw that his father had placed his right hand on Ephraim’s head, he was displeased and took his father’s hand to move it from Ephraim’s head to Manasseh’s. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>“Not so, my father!” Joseph said. “This one is the firstborn; put your right hand on his head.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>But his father refused. “I know, my son, I know!” he said. “He too shall become a people, and he too shall be great; nevertheless, his younger brother shall be greater than he, and his offspring shall become a multitude of nations.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>So that day Jacob blessed them and said: 
“By you shall Israel pronounce this blessing: 
‘May God make you like Ephraim and Manasseh.’” 
So he put Ephraim before Manasseh. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Then Israel said to Joseph, “Look, I am about to die, but God will be with you and bring you back to the land of your fathers. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_48_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>And to you, as one who is above your brothers, I give the ridge of land <span> <a href="#genesis_48_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
that I took from the Amorites with my sword and bow.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_48_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_48_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>48:3</em>: Hebrew El-Shaddai </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_48_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>48:7</em>: That is, northwest Mesopotamia </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_48_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>48:16</em>: Or Angel </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_48_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>48:22</em>: Or one portion of the land; Hebrew shekem, which sounds like the town and district called Shechem </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_49"> 49 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_49_front">
<h3> Jacob Blesses His Sons
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob called for his sons and said, “Gather around so that I can tell you what will happen to you in the days to come: 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>Come together and listen, O sons of Jacob; 
listen to your father Israel. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>Reuben, you are my firstborn, my might, 
and the beginning of my strength, 
excelling in honor, 
excelling in power. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>Uncontrolled as the waters, 
you will no longer excel, 
because you went up to your father’s bed, 
onto my couch, and defiled it. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>Simeon and Levi are brothers; 
their swords <span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>
are weapons of violence. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>May I never enter their council; 
may I never join their assembly. 
For they kill men in their anger, 
and hamstring oxen on a whim. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Cursed be their anger, for it is strong, 
and their wrath, for it is cruel! 
I will disperse them in Jacob 
and scatter them in Israel. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>Judah,<span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
your brothers shall praise you. 
Your hand shall be on the necks of your enemies; 
your father’s sons shall bow down to you. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Judah is a young lion—
my son, you return from the prey. 
Like a lion he crouches and lies down; 
like a lioness, who dares to rouse him? 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>The scepter will not depart from Judah, 
nor the staff from between his feet,<span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_3">[3]</a> </span>

until Shiloh <span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_4">[4]</a> </span>
comes 
and the allegiance of the nations is his. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>He ties his donkey to the vine, 
his colt to the choicest branch. 
He washes his garments in wine, 
his robes in the blood of grapes. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>His eyes are darker than wine, 
and his teeth are whiter than milk. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>Zebulun shall dwell by the seashore 
and become a harbor for ships; 
his border shall extend to Sidon. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>Issachar is a strong donkey, 
lying down between the sheepfolds.<span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_5">[5]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>He saw that his resting place was good 
and that his land was pleasant, 
so he bent his shoulder to the burden 
and submitted to labor as a servant. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>Dan shall provide justice for his people <span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_6">[6]</a> </span>

as one of the tribes of Israel. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>He will be a snake by the road, 
a viper in the path 
that bites the horse’s heels 
so that its rider tumbles backward. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>I await Your salvation, O LORD. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>Gad <span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_7">[7]</a> </span>
will be attacked by raiders, 
but he will attack their heels. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>Asher’s food will be rich; 
he shall provide royal delicacies. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Naphtali is a doe set free 
that bears beautiful fawns.<span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_8">[8]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Joseph is a fruitful vine—
a fruitful vine by a spring, 
whose branches scale the wall.<span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_9">[9]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>The archers attacked him with bitterness; 
they aimed at him in hostility. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Yet he steadied his bow, 
and his strong arms were tempered 
by the hands of the Mighty One of Jacob, 
in the name of the Shepherd, the Rock of Israel, 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>by the God of your father who helps you, 
and by the Almighty <span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_10">[10]</a> </span>
who blesses you, 
with blessings of the heavens above, 
with blessings of the depths below, 
with blessings of the breasts and womb. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>The blessings of your father have surpassed 
the blessings of the ancient mountains <span> <a href="#genesis_49_footnote_11">[11]</a> </span>

and the bounty of the everlasting hills. 
May they rest on the head of Joseph, 
on the brow of the prince of his brothers. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_27"> 27 </a> 
<div>Benjamin is a ravenous wolf; 
in the morning he devours the prey, 
in the evening he divides the plunder.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_28"> 28 </a> 
<div>These are the tribes of Israel, twelve in all, and this was what their father said to them. He blessed them, and he blessed each one with a suitable blessing. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_49_28_section">
<h3> The Death of Jacob
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_29"> 29 </a> 
<div>Then Jacob instructed them, “I am about to be gathered to my people. Bury me with my fathers in the cave in the field of Ephron the Hittite. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_30"> 30 </a> 
<div>The cave is in the field of Machpelah near Mamre, in the land of Canaan. This is the field Abraham purchased from Ephron the Hittite as a burial site. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_31"> 31 </a> 
<div>There Abraham and his wife Sarah are buried, there Isaac and his wife Rebekah are buried, and there I buried Leah. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_32"> 32 </a> 
<div>The field and the cave that is in it were purchased from the Hittites.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_49_33"> 33 </a> 
<div>When Jacob had finished instructing his sons, he pulled his feet into the bed and breathed his last, and he was gathered to his people. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_49_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>49:5</em>: Or dwelling places </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>49:8</em>: Judah sounds like the Hebrew for praise. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_3>[3]</a> <em>49:10</em>: Or from his descendants </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_4>[4]</a> <em>49:10</em>: Or until the one to whom tribute belongs </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_5>[5]</a> <em>49:14</em>: Or saddlebags or hearths </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_6>[6]</a> <em>49:16</em>: Or Dan shall judge his people; Dan means He has judged or He has vindicated. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_7>[7]</a> <em>49:19</em>: Gad sounds like the Hebrew for raid and also for band of raiders. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_8>[8]</a> <em>49:21</em>: Or gives beautiful words or bears fawns of the fold </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_9>[9]</a> <em>49:22</em>: Or Joseph is a wild donkey, a wild donkey by a spring, with his wild colts beside the wall </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_10>[10]</a> <em>49:25</em>: Hebrew Shaddai </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_49_footnote_11>[11]</a> <em>49:26</em>: Or of my ancestors </div>
</div>
</div>
<div style="margin-bottom:2rem;">
<h2 id="genesis_50"> 50 </h2>
<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_50_front">
<h3> Mourning and Burial for Jacob
 </h3>
 
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_1"> 1 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph fell upon his father’s face, wept over him, and kissed him. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_2"> 2 </a> 
<div>And Joseph directed the physicians in his service to embalm his father Israel. So they embalmed him, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_3"> 3 </a> 
<div>taking the forty days required to complete the embalming. And the Egyptians mourned for him seventy days. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_4"> 4 </a> 
<div>When the days of mourning had passed, Joseph said to Pharaoh’s court, “If I have found favor in your eyes, please tell Pharaoh that 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_5"> 5 </a> 
<div>my father made me swear an oath when he said, ‘I am about to die. You must bury me in the tomb that I dug for myself in the land of Canaan.’ Now let me go and bury my father, and then return.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_6"> 6 </a> 
<div>Pharaoh replied, “Go up and bury your father, as he made you swear to do.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_7"> 7 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph went to bury his father, and all the servants of Pharaoh accompanied him—the elders of Pharaoh’s household and all the elders of the land of Egypt—
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_8"> 8 </a> 
<div>along with all of Joseph’s household, and his brothers, and his father’s household. Only their children and flocks and herds were left in Goshen. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_9"> 9 </a> 
<div>Chariots and horsemen alike went up with him, and it was an exceedingly large procession. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_10"> 10 </a> 
<div>When they reached the threshing floor of Atad, which is across the Jordan, they lamented and wailed loudly, and Joseph mourned for his father seven days. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_11"> 11 </a> 
<div>When the Canaanites of the land saw the mourning at the threshing floor of Atad, they said, “This is a solemn ceremony of mourning by the Egyptians.” Thus the place across the Jordan is called Abel-mizraim.<span> <a href="#genesis_50_footnote_1">[1]</a> </span>

 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_12"> 12 </a> 
<div>So Jacob’s sons did as he had charged them. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_13"> 13 </a> 
<div>They carried him to the land of Canaan and buried him in the cave at Machpelah in the field near Mamre, which Abraham had purchased from Ephron the Hittite as a burial site. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_14"> 14 </a> 
<div>After Joseph had buried his father, he returned to Egypt with his brothers and all who had gone with him to bury his father. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_50_14_section">
<h3> Joseph Comforts His Brothers
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_15"> 15 </a> 
<div>When Joseph’s brothers saw that their father was dead, they said, “What if Joseph bears a grudge? Then he will surely repay us for all the evil that we did to him.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_16"> 16 </a> 
<div>So they sent word to Joseph, saying, “Before he died, your father commanded, 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_17"> 17 </a> 
<div>‘This is what you are to say to Joseph: I beg you, please forgive the transgression and sin of your brothers, for they did you wrong.’ So now, Joseph, please forgive the transgression of the servants of the God of your father.” 
When their message came to him, Joseph wept. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_18"> 18 </a> 
<div>His brothers also came to him, bowed down before him, and said, “We are your slaves!” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_19"> 19 </a> 
<div>But Joseph replied, “Do not be afraid. Am I in the place of God? 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_20"> 20 </a> 
<div>As for you, what you intended against me for evil, God intended for good, in order to accomplish a day like this—to preserve the lives of many people. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_21"> 21 </a> 
<div>Therefore do not be afraid. I will provide for you and your little ones.” So Joseph reassured his brothers and spoke kindly to them. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="padding:0.5rem;" id="genesis_50_21_section">
<h3> The Death of Joseph
 </h3>
</div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_22"> 22 </a> 
<div>Now Joseph and his father’s household remained in Egypt, and Joseph lived to the age of 110. 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_23"> 23 </a> 
<div>He saw Ephraim’s sons to the third generation, and indeed the sons of Machir son of Manasseh were brought up <span> <a href="#genesis_50_footnote_2">[2]</a> </span>
on Joseph’s knees. 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_24"> 24 </a> 
<div>Then Joseph said to his brothers, “I am about to die, but God will surely visit you and bring you up from this land to the land He promised on oath to Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob.” 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_25"> 25 </a> 
<div>And Joseph made the sons of Israel take an oath and said, “God will surely attend to you, and then you must carry my bones up from this place.” 
 
</div></div>

<div style="display:flex; padding:0.5rem; gap:0.5rem; align-items:flex-start; ">
<a id="genesis_50_26"> 26 </a> 
<div>So Joseph died at the age of 110. And they embalmed his body and placed it in a coffin in Egypt. 
</div></div>

<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.5rem; border-top:1px solid #ccc; border-bottom:1px solid #ccc; margin:1rem 0; padding:1rem 0;" id="genesis_50_footnotes">
<h3> Footnotes </h3>
<div> <a id=genesis_50_footnote_1>[1]</a> <em>50:11</em>: Abel-mizraim means mourning of the Egyptians. </div>
<div> <a id=genesis_50_footnote_2>[2]</a> <em>50:23</em>: Or born or placed at birth </div>
</div>
</div>
